+#include "camera_v143.h"
+#include "../runtime/cpu.h"
+#include <math.h>
+#include <stdatomic.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <string.h>
+
+#ifndef M_PI
+#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
+#endif
+
+#define RAD_TO_DEG (180.0 / M_PI)
+#define DEG_TO_RAD (M_PI / 180.0)
+
+/* Adapter state for the active logical camera tick */
+typedef struct {
+    uint64_t p_cam;
+    uint64_t p_ctrl;
+    uint64_t cam_mode;
+    uint64_t calc_fn_addr;
+    uint32_t switcher_mode;
+    bool     stick_blocked_by_ui_or_manip;
+    bool     direct_active;
+    bool     applied_this_tick;
+    uint64_t next_tick_id;
+    uint64_t tick_id;
+    uint64_t last_tick_ns;
+    bool     has_test_time;
+    uint64_t test_now_ns;
+    bool     is_alt_5c1_branch;
+    uint64_t normal_branch_ticks;
+    uint64_t alt_branch_ticks;
+    bool     logged_first_tick;
+    bool     logged_first_alt_tick;
+    bool     logged_first_motion;
+    float    yaw_delta_deg;
+    float    pitch_delta_deg;
+    LookCommand cmd;
+} TotkCameraV143State;
+
+static TotkCameraV143State g_cam_state;
+static uint64_t g_logged_unclassified[16];
+static unsigned g_logged_unclassified_count;
+
+static inline float read_f32(uint64_t ea) {
+    uint32_t raw = LD32(ea);
+    float val;
+    memcpy(&val, &raw, sizeof(val));
+    return val;
+}
+
+static inline void write_f32(uint64_t ea, float val) {
+    uint32_t raw;
+    memcpy(&raw, &val, sizeof(raw));
+    ST32(ea, raw);
+}
+
+static inline float get_vf32(const Cpu *c, int reg) {
+    uint32_t raw = (uint32_t)c->v[reg];
+    float val;
+    memcpy(&val, &raw, sizeof(val));
+    return val;
+}
+
+static inline void set_vf32(Cpu *c, int reg, float val) {
+    uint32_t raw;
+    memcpy(&raw, &val, sizeof(raw));
+    c->v[reg] = (uint64_t)raw;
+}
+
+static inline float wrap_deg_180(float deg) {
+    if (!isfinite(deg)) return 0.0f;
+    deg = fmodf(deg + 180.0f, 360.0f);
+    if (deg < 0.0f) deg += 360.0f;
+    return deg - 180.0f;
+}
+
+static inline float clamp_f32(float val, float lo, float hi) {
+    if (!isfinite(val)) return 0.0f;
+    if (lo > hi) {
+        float tmp = lo;
+        lo = hi;
+        hi = tmp;
+    }
+    if (val < lo) return lo;
+    if (val > hi) return hi;
+    return val;
+}
+
+static inline void mark_applied_current_tick(void) {
+    if (!g_cam_state.applied_this_tick) {
+        g_cam_state.applied_this_tick = true;
+        mouse_camera_mark_applied(g_cam_state.tick_id, g_cam_state.cam_mode);
+        if (!g_cam_state.has_test_time && !g_cam_state.logged_first_motion &&
+            (fabsf(g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg) > 1e-5f || fabsf(g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg) > 1e-5f)) {
+            g_cam_state.logged_first_motion = true;
+            fprintf(stderr,
+                    "[totk-camera-v143] first mouse rotation applied: calc=%#llx branch=%s dyaw=%.3f deg dpitch=%.3f deg (normal_ticks=%llu alt_ticks=%llu)\n",
+                    (unsigned long long)g_cam_state.calc_fn_addr,
+                    g_cam_state.is_alt_5c1_branch ? "alt_5c1" : "normal",
+                    (double)g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg,
+                    (double)g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg,
+                    (unsigned long long)g_cam_state.normal_branch_ticks,
+                    (unsigned long long)g_cam_state.alt_branch_ticks);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static bool is_known_cutscene_or_disabled_calc(uint64_t calc_fn_addr) {
+    switch (calc_fn_addr) {
+        case 0x8023c4704ull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraEvent */
+        case 0x8023c4968ull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraEventRail */
+        case 0x8023c531cull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraEventSimple */
+        case 0x8023c64f4ull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraEventTalk */
+        case 0x8023c7de0ull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraEventTurn */
+        case 0x8023c89f0ull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraEventWatch */
+        case 0x8023c8ffcull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraGimmick */
+        case 0x8023c9dd4ull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraEventTalkManualCtrlRet */
+        case 0x8023cdbc8ull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraInitialize */
+        case 0x8023cdb18ull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraKeep */
+        case 0x8023c222cull: /* ExecutePlayerCameraBase (null/base stub) */
+            return true;
+        default:
+            return false;
+    }
+}
+
+static void log_unclassified_once(uint64_t calc_fn_addr) {
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < g_logged_unclassified_count; i++) {
+        if (g_logged_unclassified[i] == calc_fn_addr) return;
+    }
+    if (g_logged_unclassified_count < 16) {
+        g_logged_unclassified[g_logged_unclassified_count++] = calc_fn_addr;
+        fprintf(stderr, "[totk-camera-v143] Unclassified camera mode calc=%#llx (switcher=%u); preserving native behavior\n",
+                (unsigned long long)calc_fn_addr, g_cam_state.switcher_mode);
+    }
+}
+
+void totk_camera_v143_reset_state(void) {
+    memset(&g_cam_state, 0, sizeof(g_cam_state));
+    memset(g_logged_unclassified, 0, sizeof(g_logged_unclassified));
+    g_logged_unclassified_count = 0;
+}
+
+void totk_camera_v143_set_test_time_ns(uint64_t now_ns) {
+    if (g_cam_state.has_test_time && g_cam_state.last_tick_ns == 0) {
+        g_cam_state.last_tick_ns = g_cam_state.test_now_ns;
+    }
+    g_cam_state.has_test_time = true;
+    g_cam_state.test_now_ns = now_ns;
+}
+
+void totk_camera_v143_get_branch_counts(uint64_t *out_normal, uint64_t *out_alt) {
+    if (out_normal) *out_normal = g_cam_state.normal_branch_ticks;
+    if (out_alt) *out_alt = g_cam_state.alt_branch_ticks;
+}
+
+CameraContext totk_camera_v143_classify_context(uint64_t calc_fn_addr,
+                                                bool stick_blocked_by_ui_or_manip,
+                                                bool *out_menu_wheel,
+                                                bool *out_lock_on,
+                                                bool *out_ultrahand_manip,
+                                                bool *out_vehicle_manual_ok) {
+    if (out_menu_wheel) *out_menu_wheel = false;
+    if (out_lock_on) *out_lock_on = false;
+    if (out_ultrahand_manip) *out_ultrahand_manip = false;
+    if (out_vehicle_manual_ok) *out_vehicle_manual_ok = false;
+
+    if (stick_blocked_by_ui_or_manip) {
+        if (calc_fn_addr == TOTK_V143_CALC_SPECIAL_POWER ||
+            calc_fn_addr == TOTK_V143_CALC_ULTRAHAND) {
+            if (out_ultrahand_manip) *out_ultrahand_manip = true;
+            return CAMERA_CTX_ULTRAHAND_OBJECT;
+        }
+        if (out_menu_wheel) *out_menu_wheel = true;
+        return CAMERA_CTX_MENU_WHEEL;
+    }
+
+    switch (calc_fn_addr) {
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_CHASE:
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_TAIL:
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_CLIMB_OBJ:
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_WATERFALL_CLIMB:
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_SHIELD_SURFING:
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_WAKEBOARD:
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_ABYSS:
+            return CAMERA_CTX_FREE_ROAM;
+
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_SPECIAL_POWER:
+            return CAMERA_CTX_ULTRAHAND_LOOK;
+
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_AIMING:
+            return CAMERA_CTX_AIM;
+
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_AIMING_TELESCOPE:
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_CAMERA_FINDER:
+            return CAMERA_CTX_SCOPE;
+
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_LOCKON_TARGET:
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_LOCKON_AIMING_AT:
+            if (out_lock_on) *out_lock_on = true;
+            return CAMERA_CTX_LOCK_ON;
+
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_ULTRAHAND:
+            if (out_ultrahand_manip) *out_ultrahand_manip = true;
+            return CAMERA_CTX_ULTRAHAND_OBJECT;
+
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_HORSE:
+            if (out_vehicle_manual_ok) *out_vehicle_manual_ok = true;
+            return CAMERA_CTX_VEHICLE;
+
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_HORSE_REVOLVE:
+        case TOTK_V143_CALC_HORSE_LOCKON_EMPTY:
+            if (out_vehicle_manual_ok) *out_vehicle_manual_ok = false;
+            return CAMERA_CTX_VEHICLE;
+
+        default:
+            if (is_known_cutscene_or_disabled_calc(calc_fn_addr)) {
+                return CAMERA_CTX_DISABLED;
+            }
+            log_unclassified_once(calc_fn_addr);
+            return CAMERA_CTX_UNCLASSIFIED;
+    }
+}
+
+int totk_camera_v143_suppress_gyro(void) {
+    MouseCameraConfig cfg;
+    mouse_camera_get_config(&cfg);
+    if (cfg.backend != MOUSE_BACKEND_NATIVE) return 0;
+    return mouse_camera_should_suppress_gyro() ? 1 : 0;
+}
+
+/* Hook at 0x800874d80 (str w8, [x20, #0x418] inside f_800874c1c):
+ * Live registers:
+ *   x20 = p_ctrl (PlayerCameraController* = p_cam + 0x490)
+ *   x19 = pad_input_ptr
+ *   x21 = (p_cam->flags_34 >> 4) & 7 (bit 0 = right-stick camera look blocked)
+ *   x8  = stick_flags
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_read_stick(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t p_ctrl = c->x[20];
+    uint32_t cam_input_flags = (uint32_t)c->x[21];
+    bool stick_blocked = ((cam_input_flags & 1u) != 0);
+
+    g_cam_state.p_ctrl = p_ctrl;
+    g_cam_state.stick_blocked_by_ui_or_manip = stick_blocked;
+
+    uint64_t now_ns = g_cam_state.has_test_time ? g_cam_state.test_now_ns : mouse_camera_now_ns();
+    mouse_camera_advance_time(now_ns);
+
+    MouseCameraConfig cfg;
+    MouseCameraDiag diag;
+    mouse_camera_get_config(&cfg);
+    mouse_camera_get_diag(&diag);
+
+    if (cfg.mouse_mode != 0 &&
+        cfg.backend == MOUSE_BACKEND_NATIVE &&
+        diag.captured &&
+        diag.owner == LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE &&
+        !stick_blocked) {
+        /* Zero processed right stick on p_ctrl so stick curve/acceleration never adds to camera */
+        ST64(p_ctrl + 0x420ull, 0ull);
+        /* Clear bit 3 (0x08) and bit 5 (0x20) ("right stick neutral") so Tail/Horse stay in
+         * manual-look sub_state (sub_state=4 / sub_state=2), preventing auto-centering and
+         * slope pitch pull while mouse owns look. */
+        c->x[8] = (uint32_t)((uint32_t)c->x[8] & ~0x28u);
+    }
+
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x418ull, (uint32_t)c->x[8]);
+}
+
+/* Hook at 0x800d85e70 (str w9, [x19, #0x24] inside ExecutePlayerCameraSwitcher::execute):
+ * Live registers:
+ *   x19 = switcher
+ *   x20 = p_cam
+ *   x9  = mode_enum
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_switcher_mode(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t switcher = c->x[19];
+    uint32_t mode_enum = (uint32_t)c->x[9];
+    ST32(switcher + 0x24ull, mode_enum);
+    g_cam_state.p_cam = c->x[20];
+    g_cam_state.switcher_mode = mode_enum;
+}
+
+/* Hook at 0x8002e2340 and 0x8002e2430 (ldr x8, [x8, #0x120] inside ExecutePlayerCameraBase::execute):
+ * Live registers:
+ *   x19 = cam_mode (ExecutePlayerCamera* instance)
+ *   x22 = p_ctrl   (p_cam + 0x490)
+ *   x8  = vtable   (LD64(x8 + 0x120) is mode's calc function address)
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t vtable = c->x[8];
+    uint64_t calc_fn_addr = LD64(vtable + 0x120ull);
+    c->x[8] = calc_fn_addr;
+
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    uint64_t p_ctrl = c->x[22];
+    bool is_alt_branch = ((LD8(p_ctrl + 0x5c1ull) >> 2) & 1u) != 0;
+    g_cam_state.cam_mode = cam_mode;
+    g_cam_state.p_ctrl = p_ctrl;
+    g_cam_state.calc_fn_addr = calc_fn_addr;
+    g_cam_state.applied_this_tick = false;
+    g_cam_state.is_alt_5c1_branch = is_alt_branch;
+    if (is_alt_branch) {
+        g_cam_state.alt_branch_ticks++;
+    } else {
+        g_cam_state.normal_branch_ticks++;
+    }
+
+    bool menu_wheel = false;
+    bool lock_on = false;
+    bool ultrahand_manip = false;
+    bool vehicle_manual_ok = false;
+    CameraContext ctx = totk_camera_v143_classify_context(
+        calc_fn_addr,
+        g_cam_state.stick_blocked_by_ui_or_manip,
+        &menu_wheel,
+        &lock_on,
+        &ultrahand_manip,
+        &vehicle_manual_ok);
+
+    float base_fov = read_f32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull);
+    float offset_fov = read_f32(p_ctrl + 0x61cull);
+    float current_vfov_deg = base_fov + offset_fov;
+    if (!isfinite(current_vfov_deg) || current_vfov_deg < 5.0f || current_vfov_deg > 150.0f) {
+        current_vfov_deg = 50.0f;
+    }
+
+    bool allow_manual_look = true;
+    if (ctx == CAMERA_CTX_VEHICLE) {
+        allow_manual_look = vehicle_manual_ok;
+    } else if (ctx == CAMERA_CTX_DISABLED) {
+        allow_manual_look = false;
+    }
+
+    uint64_t now_ns = g_cam_state.has_test_time ? g_cam_state.test_now_ns : mouse_camera_now_ns();
+    double dt_sec = 1.0 / 60.0;
+    if (g_cam_state.last_tick_ns != 0 && now_ns > g_cam_state.last_tick_ns) {
+        double raw_dt = (double)(now_ns - g_cam_state.last_tick_ns) * 1e-9;
+        if (raw_dt < 0.0005) raw_dt = 0.0005;
+        if (raw_dt > 0.25) raw_dt = 0.25;
+        dt_sec = raw_dt;
+    }
+    g_cam_state.last_tick_ns = now_ns;
+
+    CameraFrame frame;
+    memset(&frame, 0, sizeof(frame));
+    frame.tick_id = ++g_cam_state.next_tick_id;
+    frame.camera_generation = cam_mode;
+    frame.context = ctx;
+    frame.now_ns = now_ns;
+    frame.dt_sec = dt_sec;
+    frame.allow_manual_look = allow_manual_look;
+    frame.current_yaw_rad = 0.0f;
+    frame.current_pitch_rad = 0.0f;
+    frame.pitch_min_rad = -(float)(85.0 * DEG_TO_RAD);
+    frame.pitch_max_rad = (float)(85.0 * DEG_TO_RAD);
+    frame.current_vfov_rad = (float)(current_vfov_deg * DEG_TO_RAD);
+    frame.neutral_vfov_rad = (float)(50.0 * DEG_TO_RAD);
+
+    mouse_camera_begin_tick(&frame, &g_cam_state.cmd);
+    g_cam_state.tick_id = g_cam_state.cmd.tick_id;
+
+    if (!g_cam_state.has_test_time) {
+        if (!g_cam_state.logged_first_tick) {
+            g_cam_state.logged_first_tick = true;
+            MouseCameraDiag diag;
+            mouse_camera_get_diag(&diag);
+            fprintf(stderr,
+                    "[totk-camera-v143] first camera tick: calc=%#llx ctx=%d branch=%s captured=%d owner=%d route=%d\n",
+                    (unsigned long long)calc_fn_addr,
+                    (int)ctx,
+                    is_alt_branch ? "alt_5c1" : "normal",
+                    diag.captured ? 1 : 0,
+                    (int)g_cam_state.cmd.owner,
+                    (int)g_cam_state.cmd.route);
+        }
+        if (is_alt_branch && !g_cam_state.logged_first_alt_tick) {
+            g_cam_state.logged_first_alt_tick = true;
+            fprintf(stderr,
+                    "[totk-camera-v143] alternate 0x5c1 branch active: calc=%#llx ctx=%d route=%d (normal_ticks=%llu alt_ticks=%llu)\n",
+                    (unsigned long long)calc_fn_addr,
+                    (int)ctx,
+                    (int)g_cam_state.cmd.route,
+                    (unsigned long long)g_cam_state.normal_branch_ticks,
+                    (unsigned long long)g_cam_state.alt_branch_ticks);
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (g_cam_state.cmd.route == LOOK_ROUTE_DIRECT_ANGLES &&
+        g_cam_state.cmd.owner == LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE) {
+        g_cam_state.direct_active = true;
+        /* TOTK spherical convention: looking right decreases yaw_deg, looking up decreases pitch_deg */
+        g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg = -(float)(g_cam_state.cmd.yaw_delta_rad * RAD_TO_DEG);
+        g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg = -(float)(g_cam_state.cmd.pitch_delta_rad * RAD_TO_DEG);
+        /* Ensure right stick on p_ctrl is zeroed and manual-look flags are active */
+        ST64(p_ctrl + 0x420ull, 0ull);
+        uint32_t f418 = LD32(p_ctrl + 0x418ull);
+        ST32(p_ctrl + 0x418ull, f418 & ~0x28u);
+    } else {
+        g_cam_state.direct_active = false;
+        g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg = 0.0f;
+        g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg = 0.0f;
+    }
+}
+
+/* ExecutePlayerCameraChase::calc (0x80018e414):
+ * Primary third-person free-roam gameplay camera (mode 0 in ExecutePlayerCameraSwitcher).
+ * 0x80018ec94: str s12, [sp, #0x48] (chase yaw_deg before spherical-to-Cartesian pos update)
+ * 0x80018edec: str s0, [sp, #0x44]  (chase pitch_deg before distance/FOV/pos update)
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_chase_yaw(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[20];
+    uint64_t p_ctrl = c->x[26];
+    float yaw = get_vf32(c, 12);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        uint32_t sub_state = LD32(cam_mode + 0x180ull);
+        bool has_mouse_delta = (fabsf(g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg) > 1e-6f) ||
+                               (fabsf(g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg) > 1e-6f);
+        if ((sub_state == 1u || sub_state == 2u) && !has_mouse_delta) {
+            /* Preserve ZL camera recenter behind Link when mouse is stationary */
+            write_f32(c->sp + 0x48ull, yaw);
+            return;
+        }
+        if (sub_state == 1u || sub_state == 2u) {
+            ST32(cam_mode + 0x180ull, 3u);
+        }
+        /* Read unperturbed spherical yaw from start of frame (sp + 0x48) so walking
+         * sideways/backwards in sub_state 3 never auto-orbits or fights the mouse. */
+        yaw = wrap_deg_180(read_f32(c->sp + 0x48ull) + g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg);
+        set_vf32(c, 12, yaw);
+        write_f32(c->sp + 0x48ull, yaw);
+        write_f32(c->sp + 0x4cull, yaw);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x120ull, yaw);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x124ull, 0.0f);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x128ull, 0.0f);
+        c->x[23] = 0ull;
+        if (p_ctrl != 0) {
+            write_f32(p_ctrl + 0x414ull, yaw);
+            if (has_mouse_delta) {
+                ST32(p_ctrl + 0x5c0ull, LD32(p_ctrl + 0x5c0ull) | 0x1u);
+            }
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+    write_f32(c->sp + 0x48ull, yaw);
+}
+
+void totk_camera_v143_on_chase_pitch(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[20];
+    uint64_t p_ctrl = c->x[26];
+    float pitch = get_vf32(c, 0);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        uint32_t sub_state = LD32(cam_mode + 0x180ull);
+        bool has_mouse_delta = (fabsf(g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg) > 1e-6f) ||
+                               (fabsf(g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg) > 1e-6f);
+        if ((sub_state == 1u || sub_state == 2u) && !has_mouse_delta) {
+            write_f32(c->sp + 0x44ull, pitch);
+            mark_applied_current_tick();
+            return;
+        }
+        /* Read unperturbed spherical pitch from start of frame (sp + 0x44) so slope/default
+         * pitch pull (f_800191970) never fights mouse vertical look. */
+        pitch = read_f32(c->sp + 0x44ull) + g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg;
+        float lo = read_f32(cam_mode + 0xf8ull);
+        float hi = read_f32(cam_mode + 0xfcull);
+        if (!isfinite(lo) || !isfinite(hi) || fabsf(lo - hi) < 1e-3f) {
+            lo = -85.0f;
+            hi = 85.0f;
+        } else {
+            if (lo > hi) {
+                float tmp = lo;
+                lo = hi;
+                hi = tmp;
+            }
+            if (lo < -85.0f) lo = -85.0f;
+            if (hi > 85.0f) hi = 85.0f;
+        }
+        pitch = clamp_f32(pitch, lo, hi);
+        set_vf32(c, 0, pitch);
+        write_f32(c->sp + 0x44ull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x108ull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x10cull, 0.0f);
+        if (p_ctrl != 0) {
+            write_f32(p_ctrl + 0x410ull, pitch);
+        }
+        mark_applied_current_tick();
+        return;
+    }
+    write_f32(c->sp + 0x44ull, pitch);
+}
+
+/* ExecutePlayerCameraTail::calc (0x8023d4950):
+ * 0x8023d4f34: ldr s0, [x19, #0xc0] (tail_cam->yaw_deg)
+ * 0x8023d5028: ldr s1, [x19, #0xb4] (tail_cam->pitch_deg)
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_tail_yaw(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    float yaw = read_f32(cam_mode + 0xc0ull);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        yaw = wrap_deg_180(yaw + g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xc0ull, yaw);
+        /* Zero auto-orbit offset and angular velocity so manual yaw is exact */
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xc4ull, 0.0f);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xc8ull, 0.0f);
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 0, yaw);
+}
+
+void totk_camera_v143_on_tail_pitch(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    float pitch = read_f32(cam_mode + 0xb4ull);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        pitch += g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg;
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xb4ull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xb8ull, 0.0f);
+        mark_applied_current_tick();
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 1, pitch);
+}
+
+/* ExecutePlayerCameraAiming::calc (0x8023be978):
+ * 0x8023bebcc: fadd s0, s1, s0 (target pitch before native mode clamps)
+ * 0x8023bec3c: bl 0x800191970  (spring damper on x19+0x15c / x19+0x160)
+ * 0x8023beeec: ldur s1, [x29, #-0x48] (yaw before native wrap & store to x19+0x158)
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_aim_pitch_add(struct Cpu *c) {
+    float s1 = get_vf32(c, 1);
+    float s0 = get_vf32(c, 0);
+    float res = s1 + s0;
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        res += g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg;
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 0, res);
+}
+
+int totk_camera_v143_on_aim_pitch_commit(struct Cpu *c) {
+    if (!g_cam_state.direct_active) return 0;
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    /* Copy freshly clamped target pitch (x19+0x154) directly to current pitch (x19+0x15c)
+     * and zero spring velocity (x19+0x160) so bow/throw aiming has zero stick damping lag. */
+    float clamped_pitch = read_f32(cam_mode + 0x154ull);
+    write_f32(cam_mode + 0x15cull, clamped_pitch);
+    write_f32(cam_mode + 0x160ull, 0.0f);
+    return 1;
+}
+
+void totk_camera_v143_on_aim_yaw(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t ea = c->x[29] - 72ull;
+    float yaw = read_f32(ea);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        yaw += g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg;
+        write_f32(ea, yaw);
+        mark_applied_current_tick();
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 1, yaw);
+}
+
+/* ExecutePlayerCameraAimingTelescope::calc (0x8023c08ac):
+ * 0x8023c0b00: ldr s0, [x19, #0x160] (target pitch before native [min,max] clamp)
+ * 0x8023c0b78: ldr s0, [x19, #0x50]  (current pitch before interpolation)
+ * 0x8023c0e80: ldr s1, [sp, #0x58]   (yaw before native wrap & store to x19+0x164)
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_scope_pitch_load(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    float pitch = read_f32(cam_mode + 0x160ull);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        pitch += g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg;
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x160ull, pitch);
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 0, pitch);
+}
+
+void totk_camera_v143_on_scope_pitch_commit(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        float clamped = read_f32(cam_mode + 0x160ull);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x50ull, clamped);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x58ull, 0.0f);
+        set_vf32(c, 0, clamped);
+        return;
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 0, read_f32(cam_mode + 0x50ull));
+}
+
+void totk_camera_v143_on_scope_yaw(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t ea = c->sp + 0x58ull;
+    float yaw = read_f32(ea);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        yaw += g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg;
+        write_f32(ea, yaw);
+        mark_applied_current_tick();
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 1, yaw);
+}

+/* ExecutePlayerCameraCameraFinder::calc (0x8023c26c8):
+ * 0x8023c28c4: str s0, [x19, #0x50] (pitch before native MinPitch/MaxPitch clamp)
+ * 0x8023c2df0: ldr s1, [sp, #0x48]  (yaw before native wrap & limit check)
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_finder_pitch(struct Cpu *c) {

+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        pitch += g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg;
+        set_vf32(c, 0, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x54ull, 0.0f);
+    }
+    write_f32(cam_mode + 0x50ull, pitch);
+}

+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        yaw += g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg;
+        write_f32(ea, yaw);
+        mark_applied_current_tick();
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 1, yaw);

+/* ExecutePlayerCameraSpecialPower::calc (0x8023d2d40):
+ * 0x8023d3158: ldr s0, [x19, #0x160] (target pitch before native clamp)
+ * 0x8023d31d0: ldr s0, [x19, #0x58]  (after spring damper 0x800191970)
+ * 0x8023d34cc: ldur s1, [x29, #-0x50] (yaw before native wrap & store to x19+0x164)

+void totk_camera_v143_on_special_pitch_load(struct Cpu *c) {

+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x160ull, pitch);
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 0, pitch);
+}

+void totk_camera_v143_on_special_pitch_commit(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        float clamped = read_f32(cam_mode + 0x160ull);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x50ull, clamped);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x58ull, 0.0f);
+        set_vf32(c, 0, 0.0f);
+        return;
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 0, read_f32(cam_mode + 0x58ull));
+}

+        write_f32(ea, yaw);
+        mark_applied_current_tick();

+/* ExecutePlayerCameraClimbObj::calc (0x8023c3a98):
+ * 0x8023c3bd8: ldr s1, [x19, #0x90] (climb yaw_deg)
+ * 0x8023c3c10: ldr s1, [x19, #0x9c] (climb pitch_deg in s0 / x19+0x98)
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_climb_yaw(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    float yaw = read_f32(cam_mode + 0x90ull);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        yaw = wrap_deg_180(yaw + g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x90ull, yaw);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x94ull, 0.0f);
+        set_vf32(c, 0, 0.0f);
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 1, yaw);
+}
+
+void totk_camera_v143_on_climb_pitch(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        float pitch = clamp_f32(get_vf32(c, 0) + g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg, -85.0f, 85.0f);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x98ull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xd8ull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x9cull, 0.0f);
+        set_vf32(c, 0, pitch);
+        set_vf32(c, 1, 0.0f);
+        mark_applied_current_tick();
+        return;
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 1, read_f32(cam_mode + 0x9cull));
+}

+/* ExecutePlayerCameraShieldSurfing::calc (0x8023d0088):
+ * 0x8023d0564: ldr s1, [x19, #0xf4]  (surf yaw_deg)

+void totk_camera_v143_on_surf_yaw(struct Cpu *c) {

+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xf4ull, yaw);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xf8ull, 0.0f);
+        set_vf32(c, 0, 0.0f);
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 1, yaw);
+}

+void totk_camera_v143_on_surf_pitch(struct Cpu *c) {

+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        pitch = clamp_f32(pitch + g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg, -85.0f, 85.0f);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xfcull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x100ull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x104ull, 0.0f);

+/* ExecutePlayerCameraWakeboard::calc (0x8023da61c):
+ * 0x8023daad0: ldr s1, [x19, #0xf0]       (wakeboard yaw_deg)
+ * 0x8023dab30: ldp s0, s1, [x19, #0xfc]   (wakeboard pitch_deg, pitch_vel)
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_wake_yaw(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    float yaw = read_f32(cam_mode + 0xf0ull);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        yaw = wrap_deg_180(yaw + g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xf0ull, yaw);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xf4ull, 0.0f);
+        set_vf32(c, 0, 0.0f);

+}

+void totk_camera_v143_on_wake_pitch(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    float pitch = read_f32(cam_mode + 0xfcull);
+    float vel = read_f32(cam_mode + 0x100ull);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        pitch = clamp_f32(pitch + g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg, -85.0f, 85.0f);
+        vel = 0.0f;
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xf8ull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xfcull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x100ull, 0.0f);
+        mark_applied_current_tick();
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 0, pitch);
+    set_vf32(c, 1, vel);
+}

+/* ExecutePlayerCameraHorse::calc (0x8023ca518):
+ * 0x8023caa38: ldr s1, [x19, #0xb4] (horse yaw_deg)
+ * 0x8023caaac: ldr s1, [x19, #0xac] (horse pitch_deg)

+void totk_camera_v143_on_horse_yaw(struct Cpu *c) {

+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        yaw = wrap_deg_180(yaw + g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x68ull, yaw);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xb4ull, yaw);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xb8ull, 0.0f);
+        set_vf32(c, 0, 0.0f);
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 1, yaw);
+}

+void totk_camera_v143_on_horse_pitch(struct Cpu *c) {
+    uint64_t cam_mode = c->x[19];
+    float pitch = read_f32(cam_mode + 0xacull);
+    if (g_cam_state.direct_active) {
+        pitch = clamp_f32(pitch + g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg, -85.0f, 85.0f);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0x64ull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xacull, pitch);
+        write_f32(cam_mode + 0xb0ull, 0.0f);
+        set_vf32(c, 0, 0.0f);
+        mark_applied_current_tick();
+    }
+    set_vf32(c, 1, pitch);
+}

+/* Fallback hook at 0x8007156c0 (bl 0x800717668 inside PlayerCamera::calc f_800715408):
+ * Live registers:
+ *   x19 = p_cam
+ *   x21 = p_ctrl (p_cam + 0x490)
+ * Applies direct spherical rotation to p_ctrl->pose_338 for any direct-camera mode
+ * (such as WaterfallClimb or Abyss) that did not hit a mode-specific hook above,
+ * right before f_800717668 and camera collision (f_80071aec4) execute.
+ */
+void totk_camera_v143_on_player_cam_calc(struct Cpu *c) {
+    if (!g_cam_state.direct_active || g_cam_state.applied_this_tick) return;
+
+    uint64_t p_ctrl = c->x[21];
+    if (p_ctrl == 0) return;
+
+    float pos_x = read_f32(p_ctrl + 0x338ull);
+    float pos_y = read_f32(p_ctrl + 0x33cull);
+    float pos_z = read_f32(p_ctrl + 0x340ull);
+    float at_x  = read_f32(p_ctrl + 0x344ull);
+    float at_y  = read_f32(p_ctrl + 0x348ull);
+    float at_z  = read_f32(p_ctrl + 0x34cull);
+
+    float dx = pos_x - at_x;
+    float dy = pos_y - at_y;
+    float dz = pos_z - at_z;
+    float dist = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
+    if (dist > 0.001f && isfinite(dist)) {
+        float pitch_deg = read_f32(p_ctrl + 0x410ull) + g_cam_state.pitch_delta_deg;
+        float yaw_deg   = wrap_deg_180(read_f32(p_ctrl + 0x414ull) + g_cam_state.yaw_delta_deg);
+        pitch_deg = clamp_f32(pitch_deg, -85.0f, 85.0f);
+
+        float pitch_rad = (float)(pitch_deg * DEG_TO_RAD);
+        float yaw_rad   = (float)(yaw_deg * DEG_TO_RAD);
+        float cos_p = cosf(pitch_rad);
+        float sin_p = sinf(pitch_rad);
+        float cos_y = cosf(yaw_rad);
+        float sin_y = sinf(yaw_rad);
+
+        float new_dx = dist * cos_p * sin_y;
+        float new_dy = dist * sin_p;
+        float new_dz = dist * cos_p * cos_y;
+
+        write_f32(p_ctrl + 0x338ull, at_x + new_dx);
+        write_f32(p_ctrl + 0x33cull, at_y + new_dy);
+        write_f32(p_ctrl + 0x340ull, at_z + new_dz);
+        write_f32(p_ctrl + 0x410ull, pitch_deg);
+        write_f32(p_ctrl + 0x414ull, yaw_deg);
+    }
+
+    mark_applied_current_tick();
+}

+#ifndef ULTRATEARS_CAMERA_V143_H
+#define ULTRATEARS_CAMERA_V143_H

diff --git a/src/host/keyboard_input.c b/src/host/keyboard_input.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..374d1f0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/host/keyboard_input.c
@@ -0,0 +1,98 @@
+/* Configurable physical keyboard/mouse buttons -> the existing guest controller ABI.
+ * Both the native defaults and launcher descriptions use keyboard_bindings.def. */
+#include <SDL3/SDL.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include <math.h>
+#include "host.h"
+#include "keyboard_input.h"
+
+typedef struct { const char *id; unsigned defaults[2], keys[2], legacy; int active; } Binding;
+#define INPUT_BINDING(id, group, sw, description, primary, secondary, legacy) \
+    {#id, {primary, secondary}, {primary, secondary}, legacy, 0},
+static Binding bindings[] = {
+#include "keyboard_bindings.def"
+};
+#undef INPUT_BINDING
+_Static_assert(SDL_SCANCODE_COUNT == 512, "input config reserves codes 512..514 for mouse buttons");
+static unsigned char held[INPUT_CODE_COUNT];
+static int mouse_mode;
+
+static int valid_key(unsigned k) {
+    return k < INPUT_CODE_COUNT && k != SDL_SCANCODE_F8 && k != SDL_SCANCODE_F11;
+}
+
+void keyboard_input_init(void) {
+    unsigned char used[INPUT_CODE_COUNT] = {0};
+    const char *mode = getenv("TOTK_CAMERA_MODE");
+    mouse_mode = mode && !strcmp(mode, "mouse");
+    float sensitivity = 1;
+    const char *s = getenv("TOTK_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY");
+    if (s) {
+        char *end; float value = strtof(s, &end);
+        if (end != s && !*end && isfinite(value) && value >= 0.1f && value <= 5.0f) sensitivity = value;
+    }
+    const char *invert = getenv("TOTK_MOUSE_INVERT_Y");
+    runtime_input_mouse_config(mouse_mode, sensitivity, invert && !strcmp(invert, "1"));
+    mouse_camera_init_from_env();
+    MouseCameraConfig mcfg;
+    mouse_camera_get_config(&mcfg);
+    memset(held, 0, sizeof held);
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < sizeof bindings / sizeof *bindings; i++) {
+        Binding *b = &bindings[i]; char name[64];
+        b->active = 0;
+        memcpy(b->keys, b->defaults, sizeof b->keys);
+        snprintf(name, sizeof name, "TOTK_KEY_%s", b->id);
+        const char *value = getenv(name);
+        if (value) {
+            unsigned a, c; char tail;
+            if (sscanf(value, "%u,%u%c", &a, &c, &tail) == 2 && valid_key(a) && valid_key(c)) {
+                b->keys[0] = a; b->keys[1] = c;
+            } else fprintf(stderr, "[totk-input] invalid %s=%s; using defaults\n", name, value);
+        }
+        for (unsigned j = 0; j < 2; j++) {
+            unsigned code = b->keys[j];
+            if (code && used[code]) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[totk-input] duplicate key %u on %s; binding cleared\n", code, b->id);
+                b->keys[j] = 0;
+            } else if (code) used[code] = 1;
+        }
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "[totk-input] custom keyboard mapping loaded; camera=%s, backend=%s, look=%.4f deg/count, aim=%.2fx, scope=%.2fx, smooth=%dms, compat-sens=%.2f, invert-y=%d\n",
+            mouse_mode ? "mouse (F8 releases/captures cursor)" : "keyboard",
+            mcfg.backend == MOUSE_BACKEND_LEGACY_STICK ? "legacy_stick" : "native",
+            mcfg.look_deg_per_count, mcfg.aim_multiplier, mcfg.scope_multiplier,
+            mcfg.smooth_ms, sensitivity, invert && !strcmp(invert, "1"));
+}
+
+int keyboard_input_mouse_mode(void) { return mouse_mode; }
+
+void keyboard_input_key(unsigned scancode, int down) {
+    if (scancode >= INPUT_CODE_COUNT || !scancode) return;
+    held[scancode] = !!down;
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < sizeof bindings / sizeof *bindings; i++) {
+        Binding *b = &bindings[i];
+        if (mouse_mode && !strncmp(b->id, "LOOK_", 5)) continue;
+        int active = (b->keys[0] && held[b->keys[0]]) || (b->keys[1] && held[b->keys[1]]);
+        if (active != b->active) { b->active = active; runtime_input_key(b->legacy, active); }
+    }
+}
+
+void keyboard_input_mouse_button(unsigned button, int down) {
+    unsigned code = button == SDL_BUTTON_LEFT ? INPUT_MOUSE_LEFT :
+                    button == SDL_BUTTON_RIGHT ? INPUT_MOUSE_RIGHT :
+                    button == SDL_BUTTON_MIDDLE ? INPUT_MOUSE_MIDDLE : 0;
+    if (code) keyboard_input_key(code, down);
+}
+
+void keyboard_input_release_mouse_buttons(void) {
+    for (unsigned code = INPUT_MOUSE_LEFT; code < INPUT_CODE_COUNT; code++) keyboard_input_key(code, 0);
+}
+
+void keyboard_input_clear(void) {
+    memset(held, 0, sizeof held);
+    for (unsigned i = 0; i < sizeof bindings / sizeof *bindings; i++) bindings[i].active = 0;
+    mouse_camera_reset(MOUSE_RESET_FOCUS_LOST);
+    runtime_input_clear();
+}

+#pragma once
+#include <stdint.h>
+enum { INPUT_MOUSE_LEFT = 512, INPUT_MOUSE_RIGHT, INPUT_MOUSE_MIDDLE, INPUT_CODE_COUNT };
+
+void keyboard_input_init(void);
+void keyboard_input_key(unsigned scancode, int down);
+void keyboard_input_mouse_button(unsigned button, int down);
+void keyboard_input_release_mouse_buttons(void);
+void keyboard_input_clear(void);
+int keyboard_input_mouse_mode(void);
diff --git a/src/host/mouse_camera.c b/src/host/mouse_camera.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..7fd7781
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/host/mouse_camera.c
@@ -0,0 +1,726 @@
+/* Generic PC mouse camera layer: raw delta accumulation, look ownership state machine
+ * (mouse vs right stick), angle conversion, FOV zoom scaling, optional exponential
+ * target-angle smoothing, and single-consumption tick lifecycle.
+ * Independent of TOTK guest memory addresses. */
+#include "mouse_camera.h"
+#include <math.h>
+#include <pthread.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#ifdef _WIN32
+#include <windows.h>
+#else
+#include <time.h>
+#endif
+
+#ifndef M_PI
+#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
+#endif
+
+#define STICK_TAKEOVER_THRESHOLD 0.25f
+#define STICK_REARM_THRESHOLD    0.15f
+#define STICK_TAKEOVER_NS        50000000ULL   /* 50 ms above 25% radial magnitude */
+#define STICK_REARM_NS           100000000ULL  /* 100 ms below 15% radial magnitude */
+#define MOUSE_TAKEOVER_COUNTS    1.0           /* 1.0 count accumulated radial motion */
+
+static pthread_mutex_t g_mc_lock = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;
+
+static MouseCameraConfig g_cfg = {
+    .version = 2,
+    .mouse_mode = 0,
+    .backend = MOUSE_BACKEND_NATIVE,
+    .look_deg_per_count = 0.04,
+    .aim_multiplier = 1.0,
+    .scope_multiplier = 1.0,
+    .smooth_ms = 0,
+    .invert_y = 0,
+    .legacy_sensitivity = 1.0f,
+    .trace_enabled = 0,
+};
+
+static LookOwner g_owner = LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE;
+static bool g_captured = false;
+static uint64_t g_epoch = 1;
+
+/* Pending raw mouse deltas (double precision) */
+static double g_pending_dx = 0.0;
+static double g_pending_dy = 0.0;
+
+/* Right-stick ownership handoff state */
+static bool g_stick_armed = true;
+static bool g_stick_in_neutral = true;
+static uint64_t g_stick_neutral_since_ns = 0;
+static bool g_stick_deflecting = false;
+static uint64_t g_stick_deflect_since_ns = 0;
+static float g_last_stick_mag = 0.0f;
+
+/* Optional exponential smoothing state (target-angle remainder in radians) */
+static double g_smooth_rem_yaw = 0.0;
+static double g_smooth_rem_pitch = 0.0;
+
+/* Tick tracking & single-consumption state */
+static MouseCameraClockFn g_clock_fn = NULL;
+static bool g_has_last_tick = false;
+static uint64_t g_last_tick_id = 0;
+static uint64_t g_last_camera_gen = 0;
+static CameraContext g_last_context = CAMERA_CTX_UNCLASSIFIED;
+static LookCommand g_current_cmd;
+static bool g_cmd_needs_apply = false;
+static bool g_cmd_applied = false;
+static bool g_compat_sampled_by_hid = false;
+static uint64_t g_unapplied_count = 0;
+static uint64_t g_applied_count = 0;
+static uint64_t g_unclassified_count = 0;
+
+void mouse_camera_set_clock_fn(MouseCameraClockFn fn) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    g_clock_fn = fn;
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+uint64_t mouse_camera_now_ns(void) {
+    MouseCameraClockFn fn;
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    fn = g_clock_fn;
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+    if (fn) return fn();
+#ifdef _WIN32
+    static LARGE_INTEGER freq;
+    LARGE_INTEGER now;
+    if (!freq.QuadPart) QueryPerformanceFrequency(&freq);
+    QueryPerformanceCounter(&now);
+    return (uint64_t)((double)now.QuadPart * (1e9 / (double)freq.QuadPart));
+#else
+    struct timespec ts;
+    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &ts);
+    return (uint64_t)ts.tv_sec * 1000000000ULL + (uint64_t)ts.tv_nsec;
+#endif
+}
+
+float mouse_camera_wrap_pi(double angle_rad) {
+    if (!isfinite(angle_rad)) return 0.0f;
+    double wrapped = atan2(sin(angle_rad), cos(angle_rad));
+    return (float)wrapped;
+}
+
+float mouse_camera_clamp_pitch(double pitch_rad, float min_rad, float max_rad) {
+    if (!isfinite(pitch_rad)) return 0.0f;
+    double lo = (double)min_rad;
+    double hi = (double)max_rad;
+    if (lo > hi) {
+        double tmp = lo;
+        lo = hi;
+        hi = tmp;
+    }
+    if (pitch_rad < lo) return (float)lo;
+    if (pitch_rad > hi) return (float)hi;
+    return (float)pitch_rad;
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_clamp_stick_vector(double vx, double vy, int32_t *out_rx, int32_t *out_ry) {
+    if (!isfinite(vx) || !isfinite(vy)) {
+        if (out_rx) *out_rx = 0;
+        if (out_ry) *out_ry = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    double mag = hypot(vx, vy);
+    if (mag > 32767.0) {
+        double scale = 32767.0 / mag;
+        vx *= scale;
+        vy *= scale;
+    }
+    int32_t rx = (int32_t)llround(vx);
+    int32_t ry = (int32_t)llround(vy);
+    if (rx > 32767) rx = 32767;
+    if (rx < -32767) rx = -32767;
+    if (ry > 32767) ry = 32767;
+    if (ry < -32767) ry = -32767;
+    if (out_rx) *out_rx = rx;
+    if (out_ry) *out_ry = ry;
+}
+
+static void clear_motion_locked(MouseResetReason reason) {
+    (void)reason;
+    g_pending_dx = 0.0;
+    g_pending_dy = 0.0;
+    g_smooth_rem_yaw = 0.0;
+    g_smooth_rem_pitch = 0.0;
+    g_cmd_needs_apply = false;
+    g_cmd_applied = false;
+    g_compat_sampled_by_hid = false;
+    g_current_cmd.route = LOOK_ROUTE_NONE;
+    g_current_cmd.yaw_delta_rad = 0.0f;
+    g_current_cmd.pitch_delta_rad = 0.0f;
+    g_current_cmd.compat_rx = 0;
+    g_current_cmd.compat_ry = 0;
+    g_epoch++;
+}
+
+static double parse_double_env(const char *name, double def_val, double min_val, double max_val) {
+    const char *s = getenv(name);
+    if (!s || !*s) return def_val;
+    char *end = NULL;
+    double v = strtod(s, &end);
+    if (end == s || *end != '\0' || !isfinite(v) || v < min_val || v > max_val) return def_val;
+    return v;
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_init_from_env(void) {
+    MouseCameraConfig cfg;
+    cfg.version = 2;
+    const char *ver = getenv("TOTK_MOUSE_CAMERA_VERSION");
+    if (ver && atoi(ver) > 0) cfg.version = atoi(ver);
+
+    const char *mode = getenv("TOTK_CAMERA_MODE");
+    cfg.mouse_mode = (mode && !strcmp(mode, "mouse")) ? 1 : 0;
+
+    const char *backend = getenv("TOTK_MOUSE_BACKEND");
+    if (backend && !strcmp(backend, "legacy_stick"))
+        cfg.backend = MOUSE_BACKEND_LEGACY_STICK;
+    else
+        cfg.backend = MOUSE_BACKEND_NATIVE;
+
+    cfg.look_deg_per_count = parse_double_env("TOTK_MOUSE_LOOK_DEG_PER_COUNT", 0.04, 0.001, 1.0);
+    cfg.aim_multiplier = parse_double_env("TOTK_MOUSE_AIM_MULTIPLIER", 1.0, 0.1, 2.0);
+    cfg.scope_multiplier = parse_double_env("TOTK_MOUSE_SCOPE_MULTIPLIER", 1.0, 0.1, 2.0);
+
+    int smooth = 0;
+    const char *sm = getenv("TOTK_MOUSE_SMOOTH_MS");
+    if (sm) {
+        int val = atoi(sm);
+        if (val == 0 || val == 5 || val == 10) smooth = val;
+    }
+    cfg.smooth_ms = smooth;
+
+    const char *inv = getenv("TOTK_MOUSE_INVERT_Y");
+    cfg.invert_y = (inv && !strcmp(inv, "1")) ? 1 : 0;
+
+    cfg.legacy_sensitivity = (float)parse_double_env("TOTK_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY", 1.0, 0.1, 5.0);
+
+    const char *tr = getenv("TOTK_MOUSE_TRACE");
+    cfg.trace_enabled = (tr && !strcmp(tr, "1")) ? 1 : 0;
+
+    mouse_camera_configure(&cfg);
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_configure(const MouseCameraConfig *cfg) {
+    if (!cfg) return;
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    g_cfg = *cfg;
+    if (g_cfg.look_deg_per_count < 0.001 || g_cfg.look_deg_per_count > 1.0 || !isfinite(g_cfg.look_deg_per_count))
+        g_cfg.look_deg_per_count = 0.04;
+    if (g_cfg.aim_multiplier < 0.1 || g_cfg.aim_multiplier > 2.0 || !isfinite(g_cfg.aim_multiplier))
+        g_cfg.aim_multiplier = 1.0;
+    if (g_cfg.scope_multiplier < 0.1 || g_cfg.scope_multiplier > 2.0 || !isfinite(g_cfg.scope_multiplier))
+        g_cfg.scope_multiplier = 1.0;
+    if (g_cfg.smooth_ms != 0 && g_cfg.smooth_ms != 5 && g_cfg.smooth_ms != 10)
+        g_cfg.smooth_ms = 0;
+    if (g_cfg.legacy_sensitivity < 0.1f || g_cfg.legacy_sensitivity > 5.0f || !isfinite(g_cfg.legacy_sensitivity))
+        g_cfg.legacy_sensitivity = 1.0f;
+
+    g_owner = LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE;
+    g_stick_armed = true;
+    g_stick_in_neutral = true;
+    g_stick_neutral_since_ns = 0;
+    g_stick_deflecting = false;
+    g_stick_deflect_since_ns = 0;
+    g_last_stick_mag = 0.0f;
+    g_has_last_tick = false;
+    g_last_tick_id = 0;
+    g_last_camera_gen = 0;
+    g_last_context = CAMERA_CTX_UNCLASSIFIED;
+    g_unapplied_count = 0;
+    g_applied_count = 0;
+    g_unclassified_count = 0;
+    clear_motion_locked(MOUSE_RESET_CONFIG_CHANGED);
+    memset(&g_current_cmd, 0, sizeof g_current_cmd);
+    g_current_cmd.owner = g_owner;
+    g_current_cmd.epoch = g_epoch;
+    g_current_cmd.suppress_gyro = (g_cfg.mouse_mode != 0);
+    g_current_cmd.suppress_gamepad_rstick = (g_cfg.mouse_mode != 0);
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_get_config(MouseCameraConfig *out) {
+    if (!out) return;
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    *out = g_cfg;
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_set_capture(bool captured) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    bool effective = captured && (g_cfg.mouse_mode != 0);
+    if (g_captured != effective) {
+        g_captured = effective;
+        clear_motion_locked(MOUSE_RESET_CAPTURE_CHANGED);
+    } else if (!effective) {
+        clear_motion_locked(MOUSE_RESET_CAPTURE_CHANGED);
+    }
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_reset(MouseResetReason reason) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    if (reason == MOUSE_RESET_INIT) {
+        g_owner = LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE;
+        g_stick_armed = true;
+        g_stick_in_neutral = true;
+        g_stick_neutral_since_ns = 0;
+        g_stick_deflecting = false;
+        g_stick_deflect_since_ns = 0;
+        g_last_stick_mag = 0.0f;
+        g_has_last_tick = false;
+        g_last_tick_id = 0;
+        g_last_camera_gen = 0;
+        g_last_context = CAMERA_CTX_UNCLASSIFIED;
+        g_unapplied_count = 0;
+        g_applied_count = 0;
+        g_unclassified_count = 0;
+    }
+    clear_motion_locked(reason);
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_push_motion(double dx, double dy, uint64_t event_ns) {
+    (void)event_ns;
+    if (!isfinite(dx) || !isfinite(dy)) return;
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    if (!g_cfg.mouse_mode || !g_captured) {
+        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+        return;
+    }
+    g_pending_dx += dx;
+    g_pending_dy += dy;
+
+    if (g_owner == LOOK_OWNER_GAMEPAD) {
+        if (hypot(g_pending_dx, g_pending_dy) >= MOUSE_TAKEOVER_COUNTS) {
+            g_owner = LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE;
+            g_smooth_rem_yaw = 0.0;
+            g_smooth_rem_pitch = 0.0;
+            g_stick_armed = false;
+            g_stick_in_neutral = false;
+            g_stick_neutral_since_ns = 0;
+            g_stick_deflecting = false;
+            g_stick_deflect_since_ns = 0;
+            g_epoch++;
+        }
+    } else {
+        /* Mouse already owns look. If the right stick is currently tilted or attempting takeover,
+         * disarm it so it must return to neutral (<15% for 100ms) before it can claim ownership. */
+        if (g_last_stick_mag >= STICK_REARM_THRESHOLD || g_stick_deflecting) {
+            g_stick_armed = false;
+            g_stick_in_neutral = false;
+            g_stick_neutral_since_ns = 0;
+            g_stick_deflecting = false;
+            g_stick_deflect_since_ns = 0;
+        }
+    }
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+static void advance_stick_timers_locked(uint64_t now_ns) {
+    if (!g_cfg.mouse_mode || g_owner != LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE || now_ns == 0) return;
+
+    float mag = g_last_stick_mag;
+    if (!g_stick_armed) {
+        if (mag < STICK_REARM_THRESHOLD) {
+            if (!g_stick_in_neutral) {
+                g_stick_in_neutral = true;
+                g_stick_neutral_since_ns = now_ns;
+            } else if (now_ns >= g_stick_neutral_since_ns &&
+                       (now_ns - g_stick_neutral_since_ns) >= STICK_REARM_NS) {
+                g_stick_armed = true;
+                g_stick_deflecting = false;
+                g_stick_deflect_since_ns = 0;
+            }
+        } else {
+            g_stick_in_neutral = false;
+            g_stick_neutral_since_ns = 0;
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (g_stick_armed) {
+        if (mag > STICK_TAKEOVER_THRESHOLD) {
+            if (!g_stick_deflecting) {
+                g_stick_deflecting = true;
+                g_stick_deflect_since_ns = now_ns;
+            } else if (now_ns >= g_stick_deflect_since_ns &&
+                       (now_ns - g_stick_deflect_since_ns) >= STICK_TAKEOVER_NS) {
+                g_owner = LOOK_OWNER_GAMEPAD;
+                g_stick_armed = false;
+                g_stick_in_neutral = false;
+                g_stick_neutral_since_ns = 0;
+                g_stick_deflecting = false;
+                g_stick_deflect_since_ns = 0;
+                clear_motion_locked(MOUSE_RESET_OWNER_CHANGED);
+            }
+        } else {
+            g_stick_deflecting = false;
+            g_stick_deflect_since_ns = 0;
+            if (mag < STICK_REARM_THRESHOLD) {
+                g_stick_in_neutral = true;
+                if (!g_stick_neutral_since_ns) g_stick_neutral_since_ns = now_ns;
+            } else {
+                g_stick_in_neutral = false;
+                g_stick_neutral_since_ns = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_advance_time(uint64_t now_ns) {
+    if (now_ns == 0) return;
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    advance_stick_timers_locked(now_ns);
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_sample_compat_stick(int32_t *out_rx, int32_t *out_ry) {
+    int32_t rx = 0, ry = 0;
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    if (g_cfg.mouse_mode && g_captured && g_owner == LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE &&
+        g_current_cmd.route == LOOK_ROUTE_COMPAT_STICK) {
+        rx = g_current_cmd.compat_rx;
+        ry = g_current_cmd.compat_ry;
+        g_compat_sampled_by_hid = true;
+    }
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+    if (out_rx) *out_rx = rx;
+    if (out_ry) *out_ry = ry;
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_notify_frame_boundary(void) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    if (g_compat_sampled_by_hid) {
+        g_current_cmd.compat_rx = 0;
+        g_current_cmd.compat_ry = 0;
+        g_compat_sampled_by_hid = false;
+    }
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_offer_stick(float rx, float ry, uint64_t event_ns) {
+    if (!isfinite(rx) || !isfinite(ry)) return;
+    float mag = hypotf(rx, ry);
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    g_last_stick_mag = mag;
+    advance_stick_timers_locked(event_ns);
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+static LookRoute select_route_locked(const CameraFrame *frame) {
+    if (!g_cfg.mouse_mode || !g_captured || g_owner != LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE)
+        return LOOK_ROUTE_NONE;
+    if (!frame)
+        return LOOK_ROUTE_NONE;
+
+    if (frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_DISABLED || !frame->allow_manual_look)
+        return LOOK_ROUTE_NONE;
+
+    if (frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_UNCLASSIFIED)
+        return LOOK_ROUTE_NONE;
+
+    if (frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_LOCK_ON)
+        return LOOK_ROUTE_NONE;
+
+    if (frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_MENU_WHEEL || frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_ULTRAHAND_OBJECT)
+        return LOOK_ROUTE_COMPAT_STICK;
+
+    if (g_cfg.backend == MOUSE_BACKEND_LEGACY_STICK)
+        return LOOK_ROUTE_COMPAT_STICK;
+
+    switch (frame->context) {
+    case CAMERA_CTX_FREE_ROAM:
+    case CAMERA_CTX_AIM:
+    case CAMERA_CTX_SCOPE:
+    case CAMERA_CTX_ULTRAHAND_LOOK:
+    case CAMERA_CTX_VEHICLE:
+        return LOOK_ROUTE_DIRECT_ANGLES;
+    default:
+        return LOOK_ROUTE_NONE;
+    }
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_begin_tick(const CameraFrame *frame, LookCommand *out) {
+    LookCommand local_out;
+    memset(&local_out, 0, sizeof local_out);
+    if (!frame) {
+        if (out) *out = local_out;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+
+    /* Guard against duplicate calls within the exact same logical tick_id */
+    if (g_has_last_tick && frame->tick_id == g_last_tick_id) {
+        local_out = g_current_cmd;
+        if (g_cmd_applied) {
+            /* Already applied on this tick: return zero delta so it cannot be applied twice */
+            local_out.route = LOOK_ROUTE_NONE;
+            local_out.yaw_delta_rad = 0.0f;
+            local_out.pitch_delta_rad = 0.0f;
+        }
+        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+        if (out) *out = local_out;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    /* Check if the previous tick had direct look motion that was never marked applied */
+    if (g_has_last_tick && g_cmd_needs_apply && !g_cmd_applied) {
+        g_unapplied_count++;
+        if (g_cfg.trace_enabled) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[totk-mouse-trace] WARNING: tick %llu (epoch %llu, ctx %d) had direct motion (yaw=%.5f, pitch=%.5f) that was not applied\n",
+                    (unsigned long long)g_last_tick_id, (unsigned long long)g_current_cmd.epoch,
+                    (int)g_current_cmd.context, g_current_cmd.yaw_delta_rad, g_current_cmd.pitch_delta_rad);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /* Context or camera generation transitions re-anchor smoothing and invalidate old state */
+    if (g_has_last_tick) {
+        if (frame->context != g_last_context) {
+            g_smooth_rem_yaw = 0.0;
+            g_smooth_rem_pitch = 0.0;
+            g_epoch++;
+            if (frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_DISABLED || !frame->allow_manual_look) {
+                g_pending_dx = 0.0;
+                g_pending_dy = 0.0;
+            }
+        } else if (frame->camera_generation != g_last_camera_gen && frame->camera_generation != 0 && g_last_camera_gen != 0) {
+            g_smooth_rem_yaw = 0.0;
+            g_smooth_rem_pitch = 0.0;
+            g_epoch++;
+        }
+    }
+
+    g_has_last_tick = true;
+    g_last_tick_id = frame->tick_id;
+    g_last_camera_gen = frame->camera_generation;
+    g_last_context = frame->context;
+    g_cmd_needs_apply = false;
+    g_cmd_applied = false;
+    g_compat_sampled_by_hid = false;
+
+    if (frame->now_ns != 0) {
+        advance_stick_timers_locked(frame->now_ns);
+    }
+
+    if (frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_UNCLASSIFIED) {
+        g_unclassified_count++;
+        if (g_cfg.trace_enabled) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[totk-mouse-trace] tick %llu: unclassified camera context (gen=%#llx)\n",
+                    (unsigned long long)frame->tick_id, (unsigned long long)frame->camera_generation);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /* If disabled / loading / cutscene / no manual look, drop pending motion */
+    if (frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_DISABLED || !frame->allow_manual_look) {
+        g_pending_dx = 0.0;
+        g_pending_dy = 0.0;
+        g_smooth_rem_yaw = 0.0;
+        g_smooth_rem_pitch = 0.0;
+    }
+
+    LookRoute route = select_route_locked(frame);
+    double snap_dx = 0.0;
+    double snap_dy = 0.0;
+
+    if (g_owner == LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE && g_cfg.mouse_mode && g_captured) {
+        snap_dx = g_pending_dx;
+        snap_dy = g_pending_dy;
+        g_pending_dx = 0.0;
+        g_pending_dy = 0.0;
+    }
+
+    local_out.owner = g_owner;
+    local_out.route = route;
+    local_out.tick_id = frame->tick_id;
+    local_out.epoch = g_epoch;
+    local_out.camera_generation = frame->camera_generation;
+    local_out.context = frame->context;
+    local_out.consumed_dx = snap_dx;
+    local_out.consumed_dy = snap_dy;
+    local_out.target_yaw_rad = frame->current_yaw_rad;
+    local_out.target_pitch_rad = frame->current_pitch_rad;
+    local_out.suppress_gyro = (g_cfg.mouse_mode != 0 && g_owner == LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE);
+    local_out.suppress_gamepad_rstick = (g_cfg.mouse_mode != 0 && g_owner == LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE);
+
+    if (route == LOOK_ROUTE_DIRECT_ANGLES) {
+        double scale_deg = g_cfg.look_deg_per_count;
+
+        if (frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_AIM || frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_SCOPE) {
+            double fov_scale = 1.0;
+            if (frame->current_vfov_rad > 0.01f && frame->current_vfov_rad < 3.05f &&
+                frame->neutral_vfov_rad > 0.01f && frame->neutral_vfov_rad < 3.05f) {
+                double num = tan(0.5 * (double)frame->current_vfov_rad);
+                double den = tan(0.5 * (double)frame->neutral_vfov_rad);
+                if (den > 1e-6 && isfinite(num / den)) {
+                    fov_scale = num / den;
+                    if (fov_scale < 0.02) fov_scale = 0.02;
+                    if (fov_scale > 2.5) fov_scale = 2.5;
+                }
+            }
+            double mult = (frame->context == CAMERA_CTX_AIM) ? g_cfg.aim_multiplier : g_cfg.scope_multiplier;
+            scale_deg *= fov_scale * mult;
+        }
+
+        double raw_yaw_deg = snap_dx * scale_deg;
+        double raw_pitch_deg = (g_cfg.invert_y ? snap_dy : -snap_dy) * scale_deg;
+        double raw_yaw_rad = raw_yaw_deg * (M_PI / 180.0);
+        double raw_pitch_rad = raw_pitch_deg * (M_PI / 180.0);
+
+        double step_yaw_rad = raw_yaw_rad;
+        double step_pitch_rad = raw_pitch_rad;
+
+        if (g_cfg.smooth_ms > 0) {
+            g_smooth_rem_yaw += raw_yaw_rad;
+            g_smooth_rem_pitch += raw_pitch_rad;
+            double tau = (double)g_cfg.smooth_ms * 1e-3;
+            double dt = (frame->dt_sec > 1e-5 && frame->dt_sec <= 0.25) ? frame->dt_sec : (1.0 / 60.0);
+            double alpha = 1.0 - exp(-dt / tau);
+            if (alpha < 0.0) alpha = 0.0;
+            if (alpha > 1.0) alpha = 1.0;
+            step_yaw_rad = g_smooth_rem_yaw * alpha;
+            step_pitch_rad = g_smooth_rem_pitch * alpha;
+            g_smooth_rem_yaw -= step_yaw_rad;
+            g_smooth_rem_pitch -= step_pitch_rad;
+            if (fabs(g_smooth_rem_yaw) < 1e-7 && fabs(g_smooth_rem_pitch) < 1e-7) {
+                step_yaw_rad += g_smooth_rem_yaw;
+                step_pitch_rad += g_smooth_rem_pitch;
+                g_smooth_rem_yaw = 0.0;
+                g_smooth_rem_pitch = 0.0;
+            }
+        } else {
+            g_smooth_rem_yaw = 0.0;
+            g_smooth_rem_pitch = 0.0;
+        }
+
+        local_out.yaw_delta_rad = (float)step_yaw_rad;
+        local_out.pitch_delta_rad = (float)step_pitch_rad;
+        local_out.target_yaw_rad = mouse_camera_wrap_pi((double)frame->current_yaw_rad + step_yaw_rad);
+        local_out.target_pitch_rad = mouse_camera_clamp_pitch((double)frame->current_pitch_rad + step_pitch_rad,
+                                                              frame->pitch_min_rad, frame->pitch_max_rad);
+        if (fabsf(local_out.yaw_delta_rad) > 0.0f || fabsf(local_out.pitch_delta_rad) > 0.0f) {
+            g_cmd_needs_apply = true;
+        }
+    } else if (route == LOOK_ROUTE_COMPAT_STICK) {
+        double dt = frame->dt_sec;
+        if (dt >= 0.001 && dt <= 0.25) {
+            double scale = 32767.0 * (double)g_cfg.legacy_sensitivity / (1000.0 * dt);
+            double vx = snap_dx * scale;
+            double vy = snap_dy * scale * (g_cfg.invert_y ? 1.0 : -1.0);
+            mouse_camera_clamp_stick_vector(vx, vy, &local_out.compat_rx, &local_out.compat_ry);
+        }
+    }
+
+    g_current_cmd = local_out;
+
+    if (g_cfg.trace_enabled) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[totk-mouse-trace] tick=%llu epoch=%llu owner=%d route=%d ctx=%d dx=%.3f dy=%.3f dyaw=%.5f dpitch=%.5f tyaw=%.4f tpitch=%.4f\n",
+                (unsigned long long)local_out.tick_id, (unsigned long long)local_out.epoch,
+                (int)local_out.owner, (int)local_out.route, (int)local_out.context,
+                snap_dx, snap_dy, local_out.yaw_delta_rad, local_out.pitch_delta_rad,
+                local_out.target_yaw_rad, local_out.target_pitch_rad);
+    }
+
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+    if (out) *out = local_out;
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_mark_applied(uint64_t tick_id, uint64_t camera_generation) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    if (g_has_last_tick && tick_id == g_last_tick_id &&
+        (camera_generation == 0 || g_last_camera_gen == 0 || camera_generation == g_last_camera_gen)) {
+        if (!g_cmd_applied) {
+            g_cmd_applied = true;
+            g_applied_count++;
+        }
+    }
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+LookOwner mouse_camera_current_owner(void) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    LookOwner o = g_owner;
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+    return o;
+}
+
+bool mouse_camera_is_captured(void) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    bool c = g_captured;
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+    return c;
+}
+
+bool mouse_camera_should_suppress_gyro(void) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    bool suppress = (g_cfg.mouse_mode != 0 && g_owner == LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE);
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+    return suppress;
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_resolve_npad_sticks(int32_t key_lx, int32_t key_ly,
+                                      int32_t key_rx, int32_t key_ry,
+                                      int32_t pad_lx, int32_t pad_ly,
+                                      int32_t pad_rx, int32_t pad_ry,
+                                      int32_t *out_lx, int32_t *out_ly,
+                                      int32_t *out_rx, int32_t *out_ry) {
+    /* Left stick: select stronger 2D vector as a unit, never mixing X and Y across devices */
+    double key_l_mag = hypot((double)key_lx, (double)key_ly);
+    double pad_l_mag = hypot((double)pad_lx, (double)pad_ly);
+    if (pad_l_mag > key_l_mag) {
+        if (out_lx) *out_lx = pad_lx;
+        if (out_ly) *out_ly = pad_ly;
+    } else {
+        if (out_lx) *out_lx = key_lx;
+        if (out_ly) *out_ly = key_ly;
+    }
+
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    if (!g_cfg.mouse_mode) {
+        /* Keyboard camera mode: select stronger 2D vector as a unit */
+        double key_r_mag = hypot((double)key_rx, (double)key_ry);
+        double pad_r_mag = hypot((double)pad_rx, (double)pad_ry);
+        if (pad_r_mag > key_r_mag) {
+            if (out_rx) *out_rx = pad_rx;
+            if (out_ry) *out_ry = pad_ry;
+        } else {
+            if (out_rx) *out_rx = key_rx;
+            if (out_ry) *out_ry = key_ry;
+        }
+    } else {
+        /* Mouse camera mode: single look owner */
+        if (g_owner == LOOK_OWNER_GAMEPAD) {
+            if (out_rx) *out_rx = pad_rx;
+            if (out_ry) *out_ry = pad_ry;
+        } else {
+            if (g_current_cmd.route == LOOK_ROUTE_COMPAT_STICK) {
+                if (out_rx) *out_rx = g_current_cmd.compat_rx;
+                if (out_ry) *out_ry = g_current_cmd.compat_ry;
+            } else {
+                if (out_rx) *out_rx = 0;
+                if (out_ry) *out_ry = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}
+
+void mouse_camera_get_diag(MouseCameraDiag *out) {
+    if (!out) return;
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_mc_lock);
+    out->epoch = g_epoch;
+    out->last_tick_id = g_last_tick_id;
+    out->unapplied_count = g_unapplied_count;
+    out->applied_count = g_applied_count;
+    out->unclassified_count = g_unclassified_count;
+    out->owner = g_owner;
+    out->captured = g_captured;
+    out->stick_armed = g_stick_armed;
+    out->pending_dx = g_pending_dx;
+    out->pending_dy = g_pending_dy;
+    out->last_cmd = g_current_cmd;
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_mc_lock);
+}

 /* nn::hid: one full-key controller (docked) or the handheld controller, fed by the keyboard and game controllers.

+static int mouse_enabled, mouse_invert_y;
+static float mouse_sensitivity = 1;
+static double mouse_dx, mouse_dy;
+static int32_t mouse_rx, mouse_ry;
+static uint64_t mouse_frame_ns;
+
+void runtime_input_mouse_config(int on, float sensitivity, int invert_y) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&input_lock);
+    mouse_enabled = on; mouse_sensitivity = sensitivity; mouse_invert_y = invert_y;
+    mouse_dx = mouse_dy = 0; mouse_rx = mouse_ry = 0; mouse_frame_ns = 0;
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&input_lock);
+}
+void runtime_input_mouse_motion(float dx, float dy) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&input_lock);
+    if (mouse_enabled) { mouse_dx += dx; mouse_dy += dy; }
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&input_lock);
+}
+void runtime_input_mouse_clear(void) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&input_lock);
+    mouse_dx = mouse_dy = 0; mouse_rx = mouse_ry = 0; mouse_frame_ns = 0;
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&input_lock);
+}
+/* Convert pixels per second to a compatibility right-stick vector once per game frame
+ * using radial vector clamping (preserving 2D direction without independent axis clipping). */
+void runtime_input_mouse_frame(uint64_t now_ns) {
+    pthread_mutex_lock(&input_lock);
+    uint64_t dt = now_ns - mouse_frame_ns;
+    mouse_rx = mouse_ry = 0;
+    if (mouse_enabled && mouse_frame_ns && dt >= 1000000 && dt <= 250000000) {
+        double scale = 32767.0 * mouse_sensitivity / (1000.0 * (dt * 1e-9));
+        double vx = mouse_dx * scale;
+        double vy = mouse_dy * scale * (mouse_invert_y ? 1.0 : -1.0);
+        mouse_camera_clamp_stick_vector(vx, vy, &mouse_rx, &mouse_ry);
+    }
+    mouse_frame_ns = now_ns;
+    mouse_dx = mouse_dy = 0;
+    pthread_mutex_unlock(&input_lock);
+}

+        SDL_SetWindowSize(window, win_w, win_h);
+        SDL_SetWindowPosition(window, SDL_WINDOWPOS_CENTERED, SDL_WINDOWPOS_CENTERED);
+        SDL_SyncWindow(window);
+    }
+    keyboard_input_init();
+    mouse_capture((SDL_GetWindowFlags(window) & SDL_WINDOW_INPUT_FOCUS) != 0);
+    if (!vkp_init(window)) fprintf(stderr, "[totk] no Vulkan presentation: the game runs without a picture\n");

+"""Direct mouse camera patches for TOTK 1.4.3 (src/host/camera_v143.c).
+
+Every patch verifies the original 32-bit ARM64 instruction word via expect() before emitting
+the replacement C statement, so lifting any other game version fails loudly.
+"""
+
+
+def expect(gen, addr, word, what):
+    got = gen.word(addr)
+    if got != word:
+        raise SystemExit(f'camera patch {what} at {addr:#x}: expected {word:#010x}, found {got:#010x} (not TOTK 1.4.3?)')
+
+
+def cam_read_stick(gen, addr):
+    """f_800874c1c (0x800874d80): zero processed right stick and set manual-look flags when mouse owns look."""
+    expect(gen, addr, 0xb9041a88, 'cam_read_stick')  # str w8, [x20, #0x418]
+    return '{ extern void totk_camera_v143_on_read_stick(Cpu *); totk_camera_v143_on_read_stick(c); }'
+
+
+def cam_suppress_gyro(gen, addr):
+    """f_800874e6c (0x800874ec0): take native inactive-gyro path (L_800874f10) when mouse owns look."""
+    expect(gen, addr, 0x54000280, 'cam_suppress_gyro')  # b.eq 0x800874f10
+    return ('{ extern int totk_camera_v143_suppress_gyro(void); '
+            'if (totk_camera_v143_suppress_gyro() || COND_EQ(c)) { goto L_800874f10; } }')
+
+
+def cam_switcher_mode(gen, addr):
+    """ExecutePlayerCameraSwitcher::execute (0x800d85e70): record active camera switcher mode enum."""
+    expect(gen, addr, 0xb9002669, 'cam_switcher_mode')  # str w9, [x19, #0x24]
+    return '{ extern void totk_camera_v143_on_switcher_mode(Cpu *); totk_camera_v143_on_switcher_mode(c); }'
+
+
+def cam_mode_pre_calc(gen, addr):
+    """ExecutePlayerCameraBase::execute (0x8002e2340, 0x8002e2430): classify camera context and begin logical camera tick."""
+    expect(gen, addr, 0xf9409108, 'cam_mode_pre_calc')  # ldr x8, [x8, #0x120]
+    return '{ extern void totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(Cpu *); totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(c); }'
+
+
+def cam_chase_yaw(gen, addr):
+    """ExecutePlayerCameraChase::calc (0x80018ec94): apply direct mouse yaw delta to Chase free-roam camera."""
+    expect(gen, addr, 0xbd004bec, 'cam_chase_yaw')  # str s12, [sp, #0x48]
+    return '{ extern void totk_camera_v143_on_chase_yaw(Cpu *); totk_camera_v143_on_chase_yaw(c); }'
+
+
+def cam_chase_pitch(gen, addr):

+    return '{ extern void totk_camera_v143_on_chase_pitch(Cpu *); totk_camera_v143_on_chase_pitch(c); }'
+
+
+def cam_tail_yaw(gen, addr):

+    expect(gen, addr, 0xbd40c260, 'cam_tail_yaw')  # ldr s0, [x19, #0xc0]

+def cam_aim_gyro_branch(gen, addr):
+    """ExecutePlayerCameraAiming::calc (0x8023be9ec): skip gyro aiming branch when mouse owns look."""
+    expect(gen, addr, 0x360002e0, 'cam_aim_gyro_branch')  # tbz w0, #0, 0x8023bea48

+"""Unit tests for TOTK 1.4.3 camera adapter (src/host/camera_v143.c) and lifter patches (camera_patches_v143.py)."""
+import pathlib
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "tools"))
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "tools" / "lifter"))
+from toolchain import CLANG64, env as tool_env  # noqa: E402
+from camera_patches_v143 import CAMERA_PATCHES_V143  # noqa: E402
