+
+HARNESS_C = r'''
+#include <assert.h>
+#include <math.h>
+#include <stdint.h>
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include "camera_v143.h"
+#include "cpu.h"
+
+volatile int g_fnprof = 0;
+void fnprof_hit(unsigned long long a, unsigned long long b, unsigned long long c) {
+    (void)a;
+    (void)b;
+    (void)c;
+}
+
+static uint32_t f2u32(float v) {
+    uint32_t u;
+    memcpy(&u, &v, 4);
+    return u;
+}
+
+static float u32f(uint32_t u) {
+    float v;
+    memcpy(&v, &u, 4);
+    return v;
+}
+
+static void check_near(float actual, float expected, float eps, const char *msg) {
+    if (fabsf(actual - expected) > eps) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "FAIL %s: actual=%.6f expected=%.6f\n", msg, actual, expected);
+        exit(1);
+    }
+}
+
+static void reset_mouse_cam(void) {
+    MouseCameraConfig cfg = {
+        .version = 2,
+        .mouse_mode = 1,
+        .backend = MOUSE_BACKEND_NATIVE,
+        .look_deg_per_count = 0.04,
+        .aim_multiplier = 1.0,
+        .scope_multiplier = 1.0,
+        .smooth_ms = 0,
+        .invert_y = 0,
+        .legacy_sensitivity = 1.0f,
+        .trace_enabled = 0,
+    };
+    mouse_camera_configure(&cfg);
+    mouse_camera_set_capture(true);
+    totk_camera_v143_reset_state();
+}
+
+static void test_context_classification(void) {
+    bool mw = false, lo = false, uh = false, vh = false;
+
+    const uint64_t free_roam_addrs[] = {
+        TOTK_V143_CALC_CHASE,
+        TOTK_V143_CALC_TAIL,
+        TOTK_V143_CALC_CLIMB_OBJ,
+        TOTK_V143_CALC_WATERFALL_CLIMB,
+        TOTK_V143_CALC_SHIELD_SURFING,
+        TOTK_V143_CALC_WAKEBOARD,
+        TOTK_V143_CALC_ABYSS,
+    };
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(free_roam_addrs) / sizeof(free_roam_addrs[0]); i++) {
+        CameraContext ctx = totk_camera_v143_classify_context(free_roam_addrs[i], false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh);
+        assert(ctx == CAMERA_CTX_FREE_ROAM);
+    }
+
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_SPECIAL_POWER, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_ULTRAHAND_LOOK);
+    assert(!uh);
+
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_AIMING, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_AIM);
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_AIMING_TELESCOPE, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_SCOPE);
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_CAMERA_FINDER, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_SCOPE);
+
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_LOCKON_TARGET, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_LOCK_ON);
+    assert(lo);
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_LOCKON_AIMING_AT, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_LOCK_ON);
+    assert(lo);
+
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_TAIL, true, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_MENU_WHEEL);
+    assert(mw);
+
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_ULTRAHAND, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_ULTRAHAND_OBJECT);
+    assert(uh);
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_SPECIAL_POWER, true, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_ULTRAHAND_OBJECT);
+    assert(uh);
+
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_HORSE, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_VEHICLE);
+    assert(vh);
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_HORSE_REVOLVE, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_VEHICLE);
+    assert(!vh);
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(TOTK_V143_CALC_HORSE_LOCKON_EMPTY, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_VEHICLE);
+    assert(!vh);
+
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(0x8023c4704ull, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_DISABLED);
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(0x8023c222cull, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_DISABLED);
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_classify_context(0x809999990ull, false, &mw, &lo, &uh, &vh) == CAMERA_CTX_UNCLASSIFIED);
+}
+
+static void test_chase_camera_direct_rotation(void) {
+    reset_mouse_cam();
+    Cpu cpu;
+    memset(&cpu, 0, sizeof(cpu));
+    uint8_t p_ctrl_buf[0x800];
+    uint8_t mode_buf[0x400];
+    uint8_t vt_buf[0x200];
+    uint8_t stack_buf[0x200];
+    memset(p_ctrl_buf, 0, sizeof(p_ctrl_buf));
+    memset(mode_buf, 0, sizeof(mode_buf));
+    memset(vt_buf, 0, sizeof(vt_buf));
+    memset(stack_buf, 0, sizeof(stack_buf));
+
+    uint64_t p_ctrl = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)p_ctrl_buf;
+    uint64_t cam_mode = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)mode_buf;
+    uint64_t vtable = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)vt_buf;
+    uint64_t sp_base = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)(stack_buf + 0x80);
+
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_CHASE);
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull, f2u32(50.0f));
+    /* p_ctrl+0x148 bit 1 (0x02) is set during normal Chase camera mode and MUST NOT block mouse look */
+    ST8(p_ctrl + 0x148ull, 0x02u);
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x410ull, f2u32(-12.0f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x414ull, f2u32(25.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0xf8ull, f2u32(-70.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0xfcull, f2u32(80.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0x180ull, 3u); /* sub_state 3 (normal chase follow) */
+
+    cpu.x[20] = p_ctrl;
+    cpu.x[21] = 0; /* cam_input_flags bit 0 == 0 (not blocked) */
+    cpu.x[8] = 0x28;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_read_stick(&cpu);
+
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(100.0, -50.0, 1500000ull);
+
+    cpu.x[19] = cam_mode;
+    cpu.x[22] = p_ctrl;
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+    assert(cpu.x[8] == TOTK_V143_CALC_CHASE);
+
+    /* Simulate ExecutePlayerCameraChase::calc prologue storing p_ctrl+0x410/0x414 to sp+0x44/0x48,
+     * followed by sub_state 3 attempting to auto-orbit v12 to 99.0f and slope-pull v0 to 0.0f. */
+    cpu.sp = sp_base;
+    cpu.x[20] = cam_mode;
+    cpu.x[26] = p_ctrl;
+    cpu.x[23] = 1ull;
+    ST32(sp_base + 0x44ull, f2u32(-12.0f));
+    ST32(sp_base + 0x48ull, f2u32(25.0f));
+    ST32(sp_base + 0x4cull, f2u32(25.0f));
+    cpu.v[12] = f2u32(99.0f); /* unwanted auto-orbit yaw */
+    totk_camera_v143_on_chase_yaw(&cpu);
+
+    cpu.v[0] = f2u32(0.0f); /* unwanted auto-pitch reset */
+    totk_camera_v143_on_chase_pitch(&cpu);
+
+    float expected_yaw = 25.0f - (100.0f * 0.04f);
+    float expected_pitch = -12.0f - (50.0f * 0.04f);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(sp_base + 0x48ull)), expected_yaw, 1e-4f, "chase sp+0x48 yaw");
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x120ull)), expected_yaw, 1e-4f, "chase cam_mode+0x120 yaw");
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(p_ctrl + 0x414ull)), expected_yaw, 1e-4f, "chase p_ctrl+0x414 yaw");
+    assert(cpu.x[23] == 0ull);
+    assert((LD32(p_ctrl + 0x5c0ull) & 1u) == 1u);
+
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(sp_base + 0x44ull)), expected_pitch, 1e-4f, "chase sp+0x44 pitch");
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x108ull)), expected_pitch, 1e-4f, "chase cam_mode+0x108 pitch");
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(p_ctrl + 0x410ull)), expected_pitch, 1e-4f, "chase p_ctrl+0x410 pitch");
+
+    MouseCameraDiag diag;
+    mouse_camera_get_diag(&diag);
+    assert(diag.applied_count == 1);
+    assert(diag.unapplied_count == 0);
+}
+
+static void test_tail_camera_direct_rotation(void) {
+    reset_mouse_cam();
+    Cpu cpu;
+    memset(&cpu, 0, sizeof(cpu));
+    uint8_t p_ctrl_buf[0x800];
+    uint8_t mode_buf[0x400];
+    uint8_t vt_buf[0x200];
+    memset(p_ctrl_buf, 0, sizeof(p_ctrl_buf));
+    memset(mode_buf, 0, sizeof(mode_buf));
+    memset(vt_buf, 0, sizeof(vt_buf));
+
+    uint64_t p_ctrl = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)p_ctrl_buf;
+    uint64_t cam_mode = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)mode_buf;
+    uint64_t vtable = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)vt_buf;
+
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_TAIL);
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull, f2u32(50.0f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x420ull, f2u32(0.75f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x424ull, f2u32(-0.50f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0xc0ull, f2u32(10.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0xb4ull, f2u32(-15.0f));
+
+    cpu.x[20] = p_ctrl;
+    cpu.x[21] = 0;
+    cpu.x[8] = 0x28;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_read_stick(&cpu);
+    assert(u32f(LD32(p_ctrl + 0x420ull)) == 0.0f);
+    assert(u32f(LD32(p_ctrl + 0x424ull)) == 0.0f);
+    assert((LD32(p_ctrl + 0x418ull) & 0x28u) == 0);
+    assert(totk_camera_v143_suppress_gyro() == 1);
+
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(100.0, -50.0, 1000000ull);
+
+    cpu.x[19] = cam_mode;
+    cpu.x[22] = p_ctrl;
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+    assert(cpu.x[8] == TOTK_V143_CALC_TAIL);
+
+    totk_camera_v143_on_tail_yaw(&cpu);
+    totk_camera_v143_on_tail_pitch(&cpu);
+
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xc0ull)), 10.0f - (100.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "tail yaw");
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xb4ull)), -15.0f - (50.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "tail pitch");
+
+    MouseCameraDiag diag;
+    mouse_camera_get_diag(&diag);
+    assert(diag.applied_count == 1);
+    assert(diag.unapplied_count == 0);
+
+    /* Fallback in PlayerCamera::calc must NOT double-apply */
+    cpu.x[21] = p_ctrl;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_player_cam_calc(&cpu);
+    mouse_camera_get_diag(&diag);
+    assert(diag.applied_count == 1);
+}
+
+static void test_aim_scope_and_fallback_spherical(void) {
+    reset_mouse_cam();
+    Cpu cpu;
+    memset(&cpu, 0, sizeof(cpu));
+    uint8_t p_ctrl_buf[0x800];
+    uint8_t mode_buf[0x400];
+    uint8_t vt_buf[0x200];
+    uint8_t stack_buf[0x200];
+    memset(p_ctrl_buf, 0, sizeof(p_ctrl_buf));
+    memset(mode_buf, 0, sizeof(mode_buf));
+    memset(vt_buf, 0, sizeof(vt_buf));
+    memset(stack_buf, 0, sizeof(stack_buf));
+
+    uint64_t p_ctrl = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)p_ctrl_buf;
+    uint64_t cam_mode = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)mode_buf;
+    uint64_t vtable = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)vt_buf;
+    uint64_t sp_base = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)(stack_buf + 0x100);
+
+    /* Aiming at zoomed vFOV = 25 deg */
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_AIMING);
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull, f2u32(25.0f));
+    ST32(sp_base - 72ull, f2u32(0.0f));
+
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(100.0, -50.0, 2000000ull);
+    cpu.x[19] = cam_mode;
+    cpu.x[22] = p_ctrl;
+    cpu.x[29] = sp_base;
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+
+    cpu.v[1] = f2u32(0.0f);
+    cpu.v[0] = f2u32(0.0f);
+    totk_camera_v143_on_aim_pitch_add(&cpu);
+    float pitch_out = u32f((uint32_t)cpu.v[0]);
+    float fov_scale = (float)(tan(12.5 * 3.14159265358979323846 / 180.0) / tan(25.0 * 3.14159265358979323846 / 180.0));
+    check_near(pitch_out, -(50.0f * 0.04f * fov_scale), 1e-4f, "aim pitch");
+
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0x154ull, f2u32(pitch_out));
+    int committed = totk_camera_v143_on_aim_pitch_commit(&cpu);
+    assert(committed == 1);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x15cull)), pitch_out, 1e-4f, "aim pitch commit");
+
+    totk_camera_v143_on_aim_yaw(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(sp_base - 72ull)), -(100.0f * 0.04f * fov_scale), 1e-4f, "aim yaw");
+
+    /* Scope (AimingTelescope 0x8023c08ac) at zoomed vFOV = 20 deg */
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_AIMING_TELESCOPE);
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull, f2u32(20.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0x160ull, f2u32(5.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0x50ull, f2u32(5.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0x58ull, f2u32(1.5f));
+    ST32(sp_base + 0x58ull, f2u32(12.0f));
+
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(100.0, -50.0, 2500000ull);
+    cpu.x[19] = cam_mode;
+    cpu.x[22] = p_ctrl;
+    cpu.sp = sp_base;
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+
+    float scope_fov_scale = (float)(tan(10.0 * 3.14159265358979323846 / 180.0) / tan(25.0 * 3.14159265358979323846 / 180.0));
+    totk_camera_v143_on_scope_pitch_load(&cpu);
+    float expected_scope_pitch = 5.0f - (50.0f * 0.04f * scope_fov_scale);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x160ull)), expected_scope_pitch, 1e-4f, "scope pitch load");
+    totk_camera_v143_on_scope_pitch_commit(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x50ull)), expected_scope_pitch, 1e-4f, "scope pitch commit");
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x58ull)), 0.0f, 1e-6f, "scope pitch vel zero");
+    totk_camera_v143_on_scope_yaw(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(sp_base + 0x58ull)), 12.0f - (100.0f * 0.04f * scope_fov_scale), 1e-4f, "scope yaw");
+
+    /* CameraFinder (0x8023c26c8) at vFOV = 25 deg */
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_CAMERA_FINDER);
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull, f2u32(25.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0x54ull, f2u32(2.0f));
+    ST32(sp_base + 0x48ull, f2u32(-8.0f));
+
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(100.0, -50.0, 2600000ull);
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+    cpu.v[0] = f2u32(3.0f);
+    totk_camera_v143_on_finder_pitch(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x50ull)), 3.0f - (50.0f * 0.04f * fov_scale), 1e-4f, "finder pitch");
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x54ull)), 0.0f, 1e-6f, "finder pitch vel zero");
+    totk_camera_v143_on_finder_yaw(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(sp_base + 0x48ull)), -8.0f - (100.0f * 0.04f * fov_scale), 1e-4f, "finder yaw");
+
+    /* Fallback spherical solver on Abyss mode (0x8023bd8d8) */
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_ABYSS);
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull, f2u32(50.0f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x338ull, f2u32(0.0f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x33cull, f2u32(0.0f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x340ull, f2u32(5.0f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x344ull, f2u32(0.0f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x348ull, f2u32(0.0f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x34cull, f2u32(0.0f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x410ull, f2u32(0.0f));
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x414ull, f2u32(0.0f));
+
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(100.0, 0.0, 3000000ull);
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+    cpu.x[21] = p_ctrl;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_player_cam_calc(&cpu);
+
+    MouseCameraDiag diag;
+    mouse_camera_get_diag(&diag);
+    assert(diag.applied_count == 4);
+    assert(diag.unapplied_count == 0);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(p_ctrl + 0x414ull)), -(100.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "abyss fallback yaw");
+}
+
+static void test_special_climb_surf_wake_and_horse_hooks(void) {
+    reset_mouse_cam();
+    Cpu cpu;
+    memset(&cpu, 0, sizeof(cpu));
+    uint8_t p_ctrl_buf[0x800];
+    uint8_t mode_buf[0x400];
+    uint8_t vt_buf[0x200];
+    uint8_t stack_buf[0x200];
+    memset(p_ctrl_buf, 0, sizeof(p_ctrl_buf));
+    memset(mode_buf, 0, sizeof(mode_buf));
+    memset(vt_buf, 0, sizeof(vt_buf));
+    memset(stack_buf, 0, sizeof(stack_buf));
+
+    uint64_t p_ctrl = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)p_ctrl_buf;
+    uint64_t cam_mode = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)mode_buf;
+    uint64_t vtable = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)vt_buf;
+    uint64_t sp_base = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)(stack_buf + 0x100);
+    ST32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull, f2u32(50.0f));
+
+    cpu.x[19] = cam_mode;
+    cpu.x[22] = p_ctrl;
+    cpu.x[29] = sp_base;
+
+    /* 1. SpecialPower (Ultrahand free look 0x8023d2d40) */
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_SPECIAL_POWER);
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0x160ull, f2u32(10.0f));
+    ST32(sp_base - 80ull, f2u32(20.0f));
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(50.0, -25.0, 4000000ull);
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+    totk_camera_v143_on_special_pitch_load(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x160ull)), 10.0f - (25.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "special pitch load");
+    totk_camera_v143_on_special_pitch_commit(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x50ull)), 10.0f - (25.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "special pitch commit");
+    totk_camera_v143_on_special_yaw(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(sp_base - 80ull)), 20.0f - (50.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "special yaw");
+
+    /* 2. ClimbObj (0x8023c3a98) */
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_CLIMB_OBJ);
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0x90ull, f2u32(30.0f));
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(50.0, -25.0, 5000000ull);
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+    totk_camera_v143_on_climb_yaw(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x90ull)), 30.0f - (50.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "climb yaw");
+    cpu.v[0] = f2u32(5.0f);
+    totk_camera_v143_on_climb_pitch(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x98ull)), 5.0f - (25.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "climb pitch");
+
+    /* 3. ShieldSurfing (0x8023d0088) */
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_SHIELD_SURFING);
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0xf4ull, f2u32(-10.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0x100ull, f2u32(8.0f));
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(50.0, -25.0, 6000000ull);
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+    totk_camera_v143_on_surf_yaw(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xf4ull)), -10.0f - (50.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "surf yaw");
+    totk_camera_v143_on_surf_pitch(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0x100ull)), 8.0f - (25.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "surf pitch");
+
+    /* 4. Wakeboard (0x8023da61c) */
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_WAKEBOARD);
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0xf0ull, f2u32(15.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0xfcull, f2u32(-4.0f));
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(50.0, -25.0, 7000000ull);
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+    totk_camera_v143_on_wake_yaw(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xf0ull)), 15.0f - (50.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "wake yaw");
+    totk_camera_v143_on_wake_pitch(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xfcull)), -4.0f - (25.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "wake pitch");
+
+    /* 5. Horse (0x8023ca518) */
+    ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_HORSE);
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0xb4ull, f2u32(45.0f));
+    ST32(cam_mode + 0xacull, f2u32(-12.0f));
+    mouse_camera_push_motion(50.0, -25.0, 8000000ull);
+    cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+    totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+    totk_camera_v143_on_horse_yaw(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xb4ull)), 45.0f - (50.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "horse yaw");
+    totk_camera_v143_on_horse_pitch(&cpu);
+    check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xacull)), -12.0f - (25.0f * 0.04f), 1e-4f, "horse pitch");
+
+    MouseCameraDiag diag;
+    mouse_camera_get_diag(&diag);
+    assert(diag.applied_count == 5);
+    assert(diag.unapplied_count == 0);
+}
+
+static void test_adapter_real_dt_smoothing_and_compat_stick_across_fps(void) {
+    const int fps_list[4] = {30, 60, 90, 120};
+    Cpu cpu;
+    uint8_t p_ctrl_buf[0x800];
+    uint8_t mode_buf[0x400];
+    uint8_t vt_buf[0x200];
+
+    /* Part A: Exponential smoothing (smooth_ms = 5) through totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc uses real dt_sec */
+    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
+        int fps = fps_list[i];
+        double dt_sec = 1.0 / (double)fps;
+        uint64_t dt_ns = (uint64_t)llround(dt_sec * 1e9);
+
+        MouseCameraConfig cfg = {
+            .version = 2,
+            .mouse_mode = 1,
+            .backend = MOUSE_BACKEND_NATIVE,
+            .look_deg_per_count = 0.04,
+            .aim_multiplier = 1.0,
+            .scope_multiplier = 1.0,
+            .smooth_ms = 5,
+            .invert_y = 0,
+            .legacy_sensitivity = 1.0f,
+            .trace_enabled = 0,
+        };
+        mouse_camera_configure(&cfg);
+        mouse_camera_set_capture(true);
+        totk_camera_v143_reset_state();
+
+        memset(&cpu, 0, sizeof(cpu));
+        memset(p_ctrl_buf, 0, sizeof(p_ctrl_buf));
+        memset(mode_buf, 0, sizeof(mode_buf));
+        memset(vt_buf, 0, sizeof(vt_buf));
+        uint64_t p_ctrl = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)p_ctrl_buf;
+        uint64_t cam_mode = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)mode_buf;
+        uint64_t vtable = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)vt_buf;
+        ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_TAIL);
+        ST32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull, f2u32(50.0f));
+        ST32(cam_mode + 0xc0ull, f2u32(0.0f));
+        ST32(cam_mode + 0xb4ull, f2u32(0.0f));
+
+        uint64_t t0 = 1000000000ull;
+        totk_camera_v143_set_test_time_ns(t0);
+        totk_camera_v143_set_test_time_ns(t0 + dt_ns);
+        mouse_camera_push_motion(100.0, 0.0, t0 + dt_ns);
+
+        cpu.x[19] = cam_mode;
+        cpu.x[22] = p_ctrl;
+        cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+        totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+        totk_camera_v143_on_tail_yaw(&cpu);
+        totk_camera_v143_on_tail_pitch(&cpu);
+
+        double actual_dt = (double)dt_ns * 1e-9;
+        double expected_alpha = 1.0 - exp(-actual_dt / 0.005);
+        float expected_first_yaw = -(float)(100.0 * 0.04 * expected_alpha);
+        check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xc0ull)), expected_first_yaw, 1e-4f, "smooth first step scales with real dt");
+
+        /* Run remaining ticks for 200ms wall-clock and verify full convergence to -4.0 deg */
+        int steps = fps / 5;
+        for (int s = 2; s <= steps + 10; s++) {
+            totk_camera_v143_set_test_time_ns(t0 + (uint64_t)s * dt_ns);
+            cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+            totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+            totk_camera_v143_on_tail_yaw(&cpu);
+            totk_camera_v143_on_tail_pitch(&cpu);
+        }
+        check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xc0ull)), -4.0f, 1e-3f, "smooth converged total angle");
+    }
+
+    /* Part B: Compat stick route in Menu Wheel through totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc is FPS-invariant */
+    int32_t ref_rx = 0;
+    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
+        int fps = fps_list[i];
+        double dt_sec = 1.0 / (double)fps;
+        uint64_t dt_ns = (uint64_t)llround(dt_sec * 1e9);
+
+        reset_mouse_cam();
+        memset(&cpu, 0, sizeof(cpu));
+        memset(p_ctrl_buf, 0, sizeof(p_ctrl_buf));
+        memset(mode_buf, 0, sizeof(mode_buf));
+        memset(vt_buf, 0, sizeof(vt_buf));
+        uint64_t p_ctrl = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)p_ctrl_buf;
+        uint64_t cam_mode = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)mode_buf;
+        uint64_t vtable = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)vt_buf;
+        ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_TAIL);
+        ST32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull, f2u32(50.0f));
+
+        /* Block stick camera look (menu wheel active: cam_input_flags bit 0 = 1) */
+        uint64_t t0 = 2000000000ull;
+        totk_camera_v143_set_test_time_ns(t0);
+        totk_camera_v143_set_test_time_ns(t0 + dt_ns);
+
+        cpu.x[20] = p_ctrl;
+        cpu.x[21] = 1;
+        cpu.x[8] = 0;
+        totk_camera_v143_on_read_stick(&cpu);
+
+        /* Constant 300 counts/sec mouse velocity */
+        mouse_camera_push_motion(300.0 * dt_sec, 0.0, t0 + dt_ns);
+        cpu.x[19] = cam_mode;
+        cpu.x[22] = p_ctrl;
+        cpu.x[8] = vtable;
+        totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+
+        MouseCameraDiag diag;
+        mouse_camera_get_diag(&diag);
+        assert(diag.last_cmd.route == LOOK_ROUTE_COMPAT_STICK);
+        if (i == 0) {
+            ref_rx = diag.last_cmd.compat_rx;
+            assert(ref_rx > 9000 && ref_rx < 10500);
+        } else {
+            assert(abs(diag.last_cmd.compat_rx - ref_rx) <= 2);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void test_execute_player_camera_base_both_branches(void) {
+    /* Verify both normal (0x8002e2340) and alternate 0x5c1 (0x8002e2430) branches
+     * of ExecutePlayerCameraBase::execute consume mouse motion once and rotate Tail camera. */
+    for (int alt = 0; alt < 2; alt++) {
+        reset_mouse_cam();
+        totk_camera_v143_set_test_time_ns(5000000000ull);
+
+        Cpu cpu;
+        memset(&cpu, 0, sizeof(cpu));
+        uint8_t p_ctrl_buf[0x800];
+        uint8_t mode_buf[0x400];
+        uint8_t vt_buf[0x200];
+        memset(p_ctrl_buf, 0, sizeof(p_ctrl_buf));
+        memset(mode_buf, 0, sizeof(mode_buf));
+        memset(vt_buf, 0, sizeof(vt_buf));
+
+        uint64_t p_ctrl = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)p_ctrl_buf;
+        uint64_t cam_mode = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)mode_buf;
+        uint64_t vtable = (uint64_t)(uintptr_t)vt_buf;
+
+        ST64(cam_mode + 0x0ull, vtable);
+        ST64(cam_mode + 0x20ull, p_ctrl);
+        ST64(vtable + 0x120ull, TOTK_V143_CALC_TAIL);
+        ST32(p_ctrl + 0x35cull, f2u32(50.0f));
+        ST32(cam_mode + 0xc0ull, f2u32(0.0f));
+        ST32(cam_mode + 0xb4ull, f2u32(0.0f));
+        ST8(p_ctrl + 0x5c1ull, alt ? 0x04u : 0x00u);
+
+        mouse_camera_push_motion(100.0, -50.0, 5000000000ull);
+
+        cpu.x[19] = cam_mode;
+        cpu.x[22] = p_ctrl;
+        {
+            uint64_t ea = cpu.x[19] + 0x20ull;
+            uint64_t v = LD64(ea);
+            cpu.x[8] = v;
+        }
+        {
+            uint64_t ea = cpu.x[8] + 0x5c1ull;
+            uint64_t v = LD8(ea);
+            cpu.x[8] = v;
+        }
+        if (((cpu.x[8] >> 2) & 1) != 0) {
+            /* Alternate L_8002e23f0 branch at 0x8002e2430 */
+            cpu.x[8] = LD64(cpu.x[19] + 0x0ull);
+            cpu.x[0] = cpu.x[19];
+            totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+        } else {
+            /* Normal branch at 0x8002e2340 */
+            cpu.x[8] = LD64(cpu.x[19] + 0x0ull);
+            cpu.x[0] = cpu.x[19];
+            totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(&cpu);
+        }
+
+        assert(cpu.x[8] == TOTK_V143_CALC_TAIL);
+        totk_camera_v143_on_tail_yaw(&cpu);
+        totk_camera_v143_on_tail_pitch(&cpu);
+
+        check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xc0ull)), -4.0f, 1e-4f, "branch tail yaw");
+        check_near(u32f(LD32(cam_mode + 0xb4ull)), -2.0f, 1e-4f, "branch tail pitch");
+
+        MouseCameraDiag diag;
+        mouse_camera_get_diag(&diag);
+        assert(diag.applied_count == 1);
+        assert(diag.unapplied_count == 0);
+        check_near((float)diag.last_cmd.consumed_dx, 100.0f, 1e-4f, "branch consumed_dx");
+        check_near((float)diag.last_cmd.consumed_dy, -50.0f, 1e-4f, "branch consumed_dy");
+
+        uint64_t normal_ticks = 0, alt_ticks = 0;
+        totk_camera_v143_get_branch_counts(&normal_ticks, &alt_ticks);
+        if (alt == 0) {
+            assert(normal_ticks == 1 && alt_ticks == 0);
+        } else {
+            assert(normal_ticks == 0 && alt_ticks == 1);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+int main(void) {
+    test_context_classification();
+    test_chase_camera_direct_rotation();
+    test_tail_camera_direct_rotation();
+    test_aim_scope_and_fallback_spherical();
+    test_special_climb_surf_wake_and_horse_hooks();
+    test_adapter_real_dt_smoothing_and_compat_stick_across_fps();
+    test_execute_player_camera_base_both_branches();
+    puts("ALL_CAMERA_V143_TESTS_PASSED");
+    return 0;
+}

+class TestCameraV143(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_01_all_lifter_patch_words_match_totk_143_main(self):
+        """Every patch in CAMERA_PATCHES_V143 must match the exact instruction word in data/exefs/main."""
+        from nso import Nso
+
+        class _NsoWordReader:
+            def __init__(self, path, base):
+                self.nso = Nso(path)
+                self.base = base
+
+            def word(self, addr):
+                return self.nso.u32(addr - self.base)
+
+        m = _NsoWordReader(str(ROOT / "data/exefs/main"), 0x800000000)
+        self.assertEqual(len(CAMERA_PATCHES_V143), 33)
+        self.assertIn(0x8002e2340, CAMERA_PATCHES_V143)
+        self.assertIn(0x8002e2430, CAMERA_PATCHES_V143)
+        self.assertIn(0x80018ec94, CAMERA_PATCHES_V143)
+        self.assertIn(0x80018edec, CAMERA_PATCHES_V143)
+        for addr, patch_fn in CAMERA_PATCHES_V143.items():
+            code = patch_fn(m, addr)
+            self.assertTrue(code.startswith("{"))
+            self.assertTrue(code.endswith("}"))
+
+    def test_03_generated_main_164_hooks_both_execute_branches(self):
+        """Both normal (0x8002e2340) and alternate (0x8002e2430) branches in f_8002e228c must call totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc."""
+        main_164 = (ROOT / "data/generated/main/main_164.c").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        fn_start = main_164.index("void f_8002e228c(Cpu *c)")
+        fn_end = main_164.index("void f_8002e2448(Cpu *c)", fn_start)
+        fn_body = main_164[fn_start:fn_end]
+        hook_stmt = "{ extern void totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(Cpu *); totk_camera_v143_on_mode_pre_calc(c); }"
+        self.assertEqual(fn_body.count(hook_stmt), 2)
+        self.assertNotIn("c->x[8] + 0x120ull", fn_body)
+
+    def test_04_generated_main_104_hooks_chase_yaw_and_pitch(self):
+        """ExecutePlayerCameraChase::calc (f_80018e414 in main_104.c) must call both chase yaw and pitch hooks."""
+        main_104 = (ROOT / "data/generated/main/main_104.c").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+        self.assertIn("{ extern void totk_camera_v143_on_chase_yaw(Cpu *); totk_camera_v143_on_chase_yaw(c); }", main_104)
+        self.assertIn("{ extern void totk_camera_v143_on_chase_pitch(Cpu *); totk_camera_v143_on_chase_pitch(c); }", main_104)
+
+    def test_02_c_adapter_hooks_and_classification(self):
+        """Compile and run C test suite for src/host/camera_v143.c with -Wall -Wextra -Werror."""
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
+            tmp = pathlib.Path(tmpdir)
+            harness_c = tmp / "harness.c"
+            harness_exe = tmp / "harness.exe"
+            harness_c.write_text(HARNESS_C, encoding="utf-8")
+
+            clang = CLANG64 / "clang.exe"
+            cmd = [
+                str(clang),
+                "-O2",
+                "-pthread",
+                "-Wall",
+                "-Wextra",
+                "-Werror",
+                "-I" + str(ROOT / "src/host"),
+                "-I" + str(ROOT / "src/runtime"),
+                str(ROOT / "src/host/mouse_camera.c"),
+                str(ROOT / "src/host/camera_v143.c"),
+                str(harness_c),
+                "-o",
+                str(harness_exe),
+            ]
+            subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT, env=tool_env(), check=True)
+            res = subprocess.run([str(harness_exe)], cwd=ROOT, env=tool_env(), capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
+            self.assertIn("ALL_CAMERA_V143_TESTS_PASSED", res.stdout)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+"""Real Qt key capture, input-profile persistence and process-environment tests."""
+import os
+import pathlib
+import sys
+import unittest
+import uuid
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+os.environ['QT_QPA_PLATFORM'] = 'offscreen'
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+import prelauncher as launcher
+from controls_widgets import event_scancode
+from input_config import ACTIONS, InputSettings
+from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QEvent
+from PyQt6.QtGui import QKeyEvent
+from PyQt6.QtTest import QTest
+from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QMessageBox
+
+
+class ControlsTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls): cls.app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.work = ROOT / 'build' / ('controls-test-' + uuid.uuid4().hex)
+        self.work.mkdir(mode=0o777)
+        self.config = self.work / 'ultratears.cfg'
+        self.config.write_text('# Preserve\nromfs=data/romfs\nTOTK_UNCAPPED=1\nTOTK_UNCAPPED_HZ=120\n')
+        self.exe = self.work / 'fixture.exe'
+        self.exe.touch()
+        self.patchers = [patch.object(launcher, 'CFG_PATH', self.config),
+                         patch.object(launcher, 'LOG_PATH', self.work / 'test.log'),
+                         patch.object(launcher, 'EXE_PATH', self.exe),
+                         patch.object(launcher, 'is_controller_connected', return_value=False)]
+        for item in self.patchers: item.start()
+        self.windows = []
+        self.window = self.open_window()
+        self.page = self.window.controls_page
+        self.window.pages_stack.setCurrentIndex(2)
+        self.window.show()
+        self.app.processEvents()
+
+    def open_window(self):
+        window = launcher.PrelauncherWindow()
+        self.windows.append(window)
+        return window
+
+    def tearDown(self):
+        for window in self.windows: window.close()
+        for item in reversed(self.patchers): item.stop()
+        for file in self.work.iterdir(): file.unlink()
+        self.work.rmdir()
+
+    def capture(self, action, slot, key):
+        button = self.page.buttons[action][slot]
+        QTest.mouseClick(button, Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton)
+        self.assertTrue(button.waiting)
+        QTest.keyClick(button, key)
+        return button
+
+    def test_key_capture_saves_and_restores_actual_scancodes(self):
+        self.capture('A', 0, Qt.Key.Key_O)
+        saved = launcher.read_config()
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_KEY_A'], '18,0')
+        restored = self.open_window()
+        self.assertEqual(restored.controls_page.buttons['A'][0].text(), 'O')
+        self.assertEqual(restored.runtime_settings()['TOTK_KEY_A'], '18,0')
+        self.assertEqual(restored.runtime_settings()['TOTK_UNCAPPED_HZ'], '120')
+
+    def test_conflict_can_be_cancelled_or_moved_without_duplicate_bindings(self):
+        with patch.object(QMessageBox, 'question', return_value=QMessageBox.StandardButton.No):
+            self.capture('A', 0, Qt.Key.Key_Space)
+        self.assertEqual(self.window.input_settings.bindings['A'][0], 8)
+        with patch.object(QMessageBox, 'question', return_value=QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes):
+            self.capture('A', 0, Qt.Key.Key_Space)
+        saved = launcher.read_config()
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_KEY_A'], '44,0')
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_KEY_X'], '0,0')
+        self.assertEqual(self.page.buttons['X'][0].text(), 'Unbound')
+
+    def test_alternative_key_and_right_click_clear(self):
+        self.capture('A', 1, Qt.Key.Key_I)
+        QTest.mouseClick(self.page.buttons['A'][0], Qt.MouseButton.RightButton)
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.read_config()['TOTK_KEY_A'], '0,12')
+        self.assertEqual(self.page.buttons['A'][0].text(), 'Unbound')
+
+    def test_tab_is_captured_and_fullscreen_capture_shortcuts_are_reserved(self):
+        with patch.object(QMessageBox, 'question', return_value=QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes):
+            self.capture('A', 0, Qt.Key.Key_Tab)
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.read_config()['TOTK_KEY_A'], '43,0')
+        for key in (Qt.Key.Key_F8, Qt.Key.Key_F11):
+            self.capture('A', 0, key)
+            self.assertIn('reserved', self.page.status.text())
+            self.assertEqual(launcher.read_config()['TOTK_KEY_A'], '43,0')
+
+    def test_windows_physical_keys_work_with_non_latin_and_extended_keys(self):
+        def event(key, scan, vk):
+            return QKeyEvent(QEvent.Type.KeyPress, key, Qt.KeyboardModifier.NoModifier, scan, vk, 0)
+        self.assertEqual(event_scancode(event(0x634, 0x1e, 0x41)), 4)  # Arabic layout, physical A
+        self.assertEqual(event_scancode(event(Qt.Key.Key_Z, 0x15, 0x5a)), 28)  # physical Y
+        self.assertEqual(event_scancode(event(Qt.Key.Key_AltGr, 0x138, 0x12)), 230)
+        self.assertEqual(event_scancode(event(Qt.Key.Key_End, 0x4f, 0x23)), 89)  # numpad 1
+
+    def test_modifiers_and_escape_can_be_assigned_as_single_keys(self):
+        with patch.object(QMessageBox, 'question', return_value=QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes):
+            self.capture('A', 0, Qt.Key.Key_Control)
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.read_config()['TOTK_KEY_A'], '224,0')
+        with patch.object(QMessageBox, 'question', return_value=QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes):
+            self.capture('A', 0, Qt.Key.Key_Escape)
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.read_config()['TOTK_KEY_A'], '41,0')
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.read_config()['TOTK_KEY_B'], '225,0')
+
+    def test_mouse_buttons_can_be_captured_moved_and_restored(self):
+        for mouse_button, code in ((Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton, 512),
+                                   (Qt.MouseButton.RightButton, 513),
+                                   (Qt.MouseButton.MiddleButton, 514)):
+            button = self.page.buttons['A'][0]
+            QTest.mouseClick(button, Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton)
+            self.assertTrue(button.waiting)
+            with patch.object(QMessageBox, 'question', return_value=QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes):
+                QTest.mouseClick(button, mouse_button)
+            self.assertFalse(button.waiting)
+            self.assertEqual(launcher.read_config()['TOTK_KEY_A'], f'{code},0')
+            self.assertEqual(self.open_window().input_settings.bindings['A'][0], code)
+
+    def test_camera_mode_sensitivity_and_inversion_round_trip(self):
+        self.page.camera_mode.setCurrentIndex(self.page.camera_mode.findData('mouse'))
+        self.page.sensitivity.setValue(1.7)
+        self.page.invert_y.setChecked(True)
+        saved = launcher.read_config()
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_CAMERA_MODE'], 'mouse')
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY'], '1.70')
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_MOUSE_INVERT_Y'], '1')
+        self.assertFalse(self.page.buttons['LOOK_UP'][0].isEnabled())
+        self.assertTrue(self.page.buttons['A'][0].isEnabled())
+        restored = self.open_window()
+        self.assertEqual(restored.input_settings.camera_mode, 'mouse')
+        self.assertEqual(restored.controls_page.sensitivity.value(), 1.7)
+        restored.controls_page.camera_mode.setCurrentIndex(0)
+        self.assertTrue(restored.controls_page.buttons['LOOK_UP'][0].isEnabled())
+        self.assertEqual(restored.input_settings.bindings['LOOK_UP'][0], 82)
+
+    def test_restore_defaults_only_resets_input_preferences(self):
+        self.capture('A', 0, Qt.Key.Key_O)
+        self.page.camera_mode.setCurrentIndex(1)
+        with patch.object(QMessageBox, 'question', return_value=QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes):
+            self.page.restore_defaults()
+        self.assertEqual(self.window.input_settings.runtime_values(), InputSettings().runtime_values())
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.read_config()['TOTK_UNCAPPED_HZ'], '120')
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.read_config()['romfs'], 'data/romfs')
+
+    def test_failed_save_restores_input_and_visible_binding(self):
+        before = self.config.read_bytes()
+        with patch.object(launcher, 'write_config_values', side_effect=PermissionError('read only')), \
+             patch.object(QMessageBox, 'warning'):
+            self.capture('A', 0, Qt.Key.Key_O)
+        self.assertEqual(self.window.input_settings.bindings['A'][0], 8)
+        self.assertEqual(self.page.buttons['A'][0].text(), 'E')
+        self.assertIn('Not saved', self.page.status.text())
+        self.assertEqual(self.config.read_bytes(), before)
+
+    def test_launch_environment_contains_saved_mapping_and_camera_values(self):
+        self.capture('A', 0, Qt.Key.Key_O)
+        self.page.camera_mode.setCurrentIndex(1)
+        with patch.dict(os.environ, {'TOTK_KEY_A': '8,0', 'TOTK_CAMERA_MODE': 'keys'}), \
+             patch.object(launcher.subprocess, 'Popen') as start:
+            self.window.launch_game()
+        child = start.call_args.kwargs['env']
+        saved = launcher.read_config()
+        for key, value in self.window.input_settings.runtime_values().items():
+            self.assertEqual(child[key], value)
+            self.assertEqual(saved[key], value)
+        self.assertEqual(child['TOTK_KEY_A'], '18,0')
+        self.assertEqual(child['TOTK_CAMERA_MODE'], 'mouse')
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.page.buttons), len(ACTIONS))
+
+    def test_direct_mouse_camera_v2_settings_and_legacy_migration_backup(self):
+        # Legacy v1 config with TOTK_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY=2.50 must not alter look_deg_per_count default (0.04)
+        self.config.write_text(
+            '# Legacy v1\nromfs=data/romfs\nTOTK_CAMERA_MODE=mouse\n'
+            'TOTK_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY=2.50\nTOTK_MOUSE_INVERT_Y=1\nTOTK_KEY_A=18,0\n'
+        )
+        v1_bytes = self.config.read_bytes()
+        w = self.open_window()
+        self.assertEqual(w.input_settings.camera_mode, 'mouse')
+        self.assertEqual(w.input_settings.mouse_backend, 'native')
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(w.input_settings.look_deg_per_count, 0.04, places=5)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(w.input_settings.aim_multiplier, 1.0, places=5)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(w.input_settings.scope_multiplier, 1.0, places=5)
+        self.assertEqual(w.input_settings.smooth_ms, 0)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(w.input_settings.sensitivity, 2.50, places=5)
+        self.assertTrue(w.input_settings.invert_y)
+
+        # Modify v2 settings via Qt controls and verify migration backup .v1.bak is created
+        p = w.controls_page
+        p.look_deg_per_count.setValue(0.065)
+        p.aim_multiplier.setValue(0.85)
+        p.scope_multiplier.setValue(0.75)
+        p.smooth_ms.setCurrentIndex(p.smooth_ms.findData(5))
+
+        backup = self.config.with_name('ultratears.cfg.v1.bak')
+        self.assertTrue(backup.is_file())
+        self.assertEqual(backup.read_bytes(), v1_bytes)
+
+        saved = launcher.read_config()
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_MOUSE_CAMERA_VERSION'], '2')
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_MOUSE_BACKEND'], 'native')
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_MOUSE_LOOK_DEG_PER_COUNT'], '0.065')
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_MOUSE_AIM_MULTIPLIER'], '0.85')
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_MOUSE_SCOPE_MULTIPLIER'], '0.75')
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_MOUSE_SMOOTH_MS'], '5')
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY'], '2.50')
+        self.assertEqual(saved['TOTK_KEY_A'], '18,0')
+
+        # Switching to legacy_stick backend disables direct spinboxes and saves backend
+        p.mouse_backend.setCurrentIndex(p.mouse_backend.findData('legacy_stick'))
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.read_config()['TOTK_MOUSE_BACKEND'], 'legacy_stick')
+        self.assertFalse(p.look_deg_per_count.isEnabled())
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__': unittest.main(verbosity=2)

new file mode 100644

+"""Compile the real mapping and HID-state code and inspect the controller seen by the game."""
+import pathlib
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import unittest
+import uuid
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+from input_config import InputSettings, ACTIONS
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'tools'))
+from toolchain import CLANG64, env
+
+
+class KeyboardInputTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls):
+        cls.work = ROOT / 'build' / ('input-test-' + uuid.uuid4().hex)
+        cls.work.mkdir(mode=0o777)
+        source = r'''
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include "host.h"
+void hle_native_thunk(Cpu *c) { (void)c; }
+int runtime_handheld_mode(void) { return 0; }
+uint64_t runtime_now_ns(void) { return 1000000000; }
+_Noreturn void runtime_fail(const char *s) { fprintf(stderr, "%s\n", s); exit(2); }
+#include "mouse_camera.c"
+#include "overrides_input.c"
+#include "keyboard_input.c"
+int main(void) {
+    char line[256], name[80], value[100], setting[200]; unsigned scan, down;
+    unsigned long long now, bits; float dx, dy; int lx, ly, rx, ry;
+    initialize();
+    while (fgets(line, sizeof line, stdin)) {
+        switch (line[0]) {
+        case 'e':
+            sscanf(line + 1, "%79s %99s", name, value);
+            snprintf(setting, sizeof setting, "%s=%s", name, value); _putenv(setting); puts("ok"); break;
+        case 'i': keyboard_input_clear(); keyboard_input_init(); puts("ok"); break;
+        case 'k': sscanf(line + 1, "%u %u", &scan, &down); keyboard_input_key(scan, down); puts("ok"); break;
+        case 'b': sscanf(line + 1, "%u %u", &scan, &down); keyboard_input_mouse_button(scan, down); puts("ok"); break;
+        case 'r': keyboard_input_clear(); puts("ok"); break;
+        case 'm': sscanf(line + 1, "%f %f", &dx, &dy); runtime_input_mouse_motion(dx, dy); puts("ok"); break;
+        case 't': sscanf(line + 1, "%llu", &now); runtime_input_mouse_frame(now); puts("ok"); break;
+        case 'c': runtime_input_mouse_clear(); keyboard_input_release_mouse_buttons(); puts("ok"); break;
+        case 'p': {
+            sscanf(line + 1, "%llu %d %d %d %d", &bits, &lx, &ly, &rx, &ry);
+            RuntimeGamepadState pad = {.buttons=bits,.lx=lx,.ly=ly,.rx=rx,.ry=ry,.connected=1};
+            runtime_gamepad_set(&pad); puts("ok"); break;
+        }
+        case 's': {
+            NpadState state; uint32_t id = 0; full_states(&state, 1, &id);
+            printf("%llu %d %d %d %d\n", (unsigned long long)state.buttons, state.lx, state.ly, state.rx, state.ry);
+            break;
+        }
+        }
+        fflush(stdout);
+    }
+}
+'''
+        (cls.work / 'test.c').write_text(source)
+        subprocess.run([str(CLANG64 / 'clang.exe'), '-O2', '-pthread',
+                        '-I' + str(ROOT / 'src/host'), '-I' + str(ROOT / 'src/runtime'),
+                        str(cls.work / 'test.c'), '-o', str(cls.work / 'test.exe')], env=env(), check=True)
+        cls.process = subprocess.Popen([str(cls.work / 'test.exe')], env=env(), text=True,
+                                       stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
+
+    @classmethod
+    def tearDownClass(cls):
+        cls.process.stdin.close()
+        cls.process.wait(timeout=5)
+        for file in cls.work.iterdir(): file.unlink()
+        cls.work.rmdir()
+
+    def request(self, *args):
+        self.process.stdin.write(' '.join(map(str, args)) + '\n')
+        self.process.stdin.flush()
+        line = self.process.stdout.readline()
+        if not line: raise RuntimeError('Native input harness exited')
+        return line.split()
+
+    def state(self): return tuple(map(int, self.request('s')))
+
+    def configure(self, config=None):
+        for key, value in InputSettings(config).runtime_values().items(): self.request('e', key, value)
+        self.request('i')
+
+    def setUp(self): self.configure()
+
+    def test_default_buttons_reach_correct_switch_bits(self):
+        bits = dict(A=1, B=2, X=4, Y=8, STICK_L=16, STICK_R=32, L=64, R=128,
+                    ZL=256, ZR=512, PLUS=1024, MINUS=2048,
+                    DPAD_LEFT=4096, DPAD_UP=8192, DPAD_RIGHT=16384, DPAD_DOWN=32768)
+        for action in ACTIONS:
+            if action['id'] not in bits: continue
+            for scan in action['defaults']:
+                if not scan: continue
+                self.request('r'); self.request('k', scan, 1)
+                self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], bits[action['id']], action['id'])
+
+    def test_remap_replaces_default_and_secondary_survives_primary_release(self):
+        self.configure({'TOTK_KEY_A': '27,25'})
+        self.request('k', 8, 1)  # old E binding no longer drives A
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 0)
+        self.request('k', 27, 1); self.request('k', 25, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 1)
+        self.request('k', 27, 0)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 1)
+        self.request('k', 25, 0)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 0)
+
+    def test_quick_tap_is_reported_once_and_focus_clear_unsticks_keys(self):
+        self.request('k', 8, 1); self.request('k', 8, 0)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 0)
+        self.request('k', 26, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[2], 32767)
+        self.request('r')
+        self.assertEqual(self.state(), (0, 0, 0, 0, 0))
+        self.request('k', 26, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[2], 32767)
+
+    def test_remapped_sticks_and_opposite_directions(self):
+        self.configure({'TOTK_KEY_MOVE_UP': '28,0', 'TOTK_KEY_LOOK_UP': '75,0'})
+        self.request('k', 26, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[2], 0)
+        self.request('k', 28, 1); self.request('k', 75, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[2:], (32767, 0, 32767))
+        self.request('k', 22, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[2], 0)
+
+    def test_mouse_velocity_is_stable_across_fps_and_repeated_hid_polls(self):
+        # In native direct camera mode (default), mouse motion does NOT inject Npad right-stick deflection
+        self.configure({'TOTK_CAMERA_MODE': 'mouse', 'TOTK_MOUSE_BACKEND': 'native'})
+        self.request('c'); self.request('t', 1_000_000_000)
+        self.request('m', 10, -5); self.request('t', 1_020_000_000)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[3:], (0, 0))
+
+        # In legacy_stick compatibility mode, mouse motion produces an FPS-stable Npad right-stick sample
+        self.configure({'TOTK_CAMERA_MODE': 'mouse', 'TOTK_MOUSE_BACKEND': 'legacy_stick'})
+        for fps in (30, 60, 90, 120):
+            self.request('c'); self.request('t', 1_000_000_000)
+            self.request('m', 250 / fps, -125 / fps)
+            self.request('t', 1_000_000_000 + round(1e9 / fps))
+            first = self.state()
+            self.assertAlmostEqual(first[3], 8192, delta=1)
+            self.assertAlmostEqual(first[4], 4096, delta=1)
+            self.assertEqual(self.state(), first)
+            self.request('t', 1_000_000_000 + round(2e9 / fps))
+            self.assertEqual(self.state()[3:], (0, 0))
+
+    def test_camera_modes_sensitivity_inversion_and_release(self):
+        self.request('k', 82, 1); self.request('m', 5, -5)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[4], 32767)  # arrows mode ignores mouse
+        self.configure({'TOTK_CAMERA_MODE': 'mouse', 'TOTK_MOUSE_BACKEND': 'legacy_stick',
+                        'TOTK_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY': '2.0', 'TOTK_MOUSE_INVERT_Y': '1'})
+        self.request('k', 82, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[4], 0)  # mouse mode ignores camera keys
+        self.request('t', 1_000_000_000); self.request('m', 10, -5); self.request('t', 1_020_000_000)
+        rx, ry = self.state()[3:]
+        # Radial clamping preserves the 2:-1 direction without saturating X independently
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(rx, 29308, delta=2)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(ry, -14654, delta=2)
+        self.request('c')
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[3:], (0, 0))
+
+    def test_gamepad_input_is_preserved_and_combined_without_axis_mixing(self):
+        self.request('k', 8, 1); self.request('p', 8, 12000, 0, 22000, -18000)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state(), (9, 12000, 0, 22000, -18000))
+        # When keyboard holds W (ly=32767) and gamepad has lx=12000, ly=0, keyboard's stronger 2D vector wins without mixing lx=12000
+        self.request('k', 26, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state(), (9, 0, 32767, 22000, -18000))
+
+    def test_unbound_reserved_and_duplicate_config_match_launcher(self):
+        profile = InputSettings({'TOTK_KEY_A': '0,0', 'TOTK_KEY_B': '65,68', 'TOTK_KEY_Y': '4,0'})
+        self.configure(profile.runtime_values())
+        self.request('k', 8, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 0)
+        self.request('k', 225, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 2)  # invalid F8/F11 settings use defaults
+        self.request('k', 4, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 2)  # duplicate A key drives movement, not Y
+
+    def test_native_loader_handles_invalid_and_duplicate_hand_edited_keys(self):
+        for invalid in ('not-a-key', '65,0', '68,0', '515,0', '-1,0'):
+            self.request('e', 'TOTK_KEY_A', invalid)
+            self.request('i'); self.request('k', 8, 1)
+            self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 1)
+        self.request('e', 'TOTK_KEY_A', '4,0')
+        self.request('i'); self.request('k', 4, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 0)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[1], -32767)
+
+    def test_shift_sprint_and_space_jump_work_together_while_moving(self):
+        self.request('k', 26, 1)   # W
+        self.request('k', 225, 1)  # Left Shift -> Switch B
+        self.request('k', 44, 1)   # Space -> Switch X
+        self.assertEqual(self.state(), (2 | 4, 0, 32767, 0, 0))
+
+    def test_mouse_buttons_drive_combat_and_release_with_cursor(self):
+        self.request('b', 1, 1)  # SDL left -> Switch Y
+        self.request('b', 3, 1)  # SDL right -> Switch ZR
+        self.request('b', 2, 1)  # SDL middle -> Switch ZL
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 8 | 512 | 256)
+        self.request('c')
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 0)
+        self.request('b', 3, 1); self.request('k', 21, 1)  # RMB + its R alternative
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 512)
+        self.request('b', 3, 0)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 512)
+        self.request('k', 21, 0)
+        self.assertEqual(self.state()[0], 0)
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__': unittest.main(verbosity=2)

+"""Unit and behavioral tests for Phase 2 (src/host/mouse_camera.c)."""
+import math
+import pathlib
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import unittest
+import uuid
+
+ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'tools'))
+from toolchain import CLANG64, env  # noqa: E402
+
+
+class MouseCameraLayerTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls):
+        cls.work = ROOT / 'build' / ('mouse-cam-test-' + uuid.uuid4().hex)
+        cls.work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        harness = r'''
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include <math.h>
+#include "mouse_camera.h"
+
+int main(void) {
+    char line[512], k[128], v[128], kv[260];
+    mouse_camera_init_from_env();
+    while (fgets(line, sizeof line, stdin)) {
+        if (line[0] == 'e') {
+            if (sscanf(line + 1, "%127s %127s", k, v) == 2) {
+                snprintf(kv, sizeof kv, "%s=%s", k, v);
+                _putenv(kv);
+            }
+            puts("ok");
+        } else if (line[0] == 'i') {
+            mouse_camera_init_from_env();
+            puts("ok");
+        } else if (line[0] == 'c') {
+            int cap = 0;
+            sscanf(line + 1, "%d", &cap);
+            mouse_camera_set_capture(cap != 0);
+            puts("ok");
+        } else if (line[0] == 'r') {
+            int reason = 0;
+            sscanf(line + 1, "%d", &reason);
+            mouse_camera_reset((MouseResetReason)reason);
+            puts("ok");
+        } else if (line[0] == 'm') {
+            double dx = 0, dy = 0;
+            unsigned long long ts = 0;
+            sscanf(line + 1, "%lf %lf %llu", &dx, &dy, &ts);
+            mouse_camera_push_motion(dx, dy, ts);
+            puts("ok");
+        } else if (line[0] == 's') {
+            float rx = 0, ry = 0;
+            unsigned long long ts = 0;
+            sscanf(line + 1, "%f %f %llu", &rx, &ry, &ts);
+            mouse_camera_offer_stick(rx, ry, ts);
+            puts("ok");
+        } else if (line[0] == 't') {
+            unsigned long long tick = 0, gen = 1, now_ns = 0;
+            int ctx = 1, allow = 1;
+            double dt = 1.0 / 60.0;
+            float yaw = 0, pitch = 0, pmin = -1.4f, pmax = 1.4f, cfov = 0.8726646f, nfov = 0.8726646f;
+            sscanf(line + 1, "%llu %llu %d %llu %lf %d %f %f %f %f %f %f",
+                   &tick, &gen, &ctx, &now_ns, &dt, &allow, &yaw, &pitch, &pmin, &pmax, &cfov, &nfov);
+            CameraFrame frame = {
+                .tick_id = tick,
+                .camera_generation = gen,
+                .context = (CameraContext)ctx,
+                .now_ns = now_ns,
+                .dt_sec = dt,
+                .allow_manual_look = (allow != 0),
+                .current_yaw_rad = yaw,
+                .current_pitch_rad = pitch,
+                .pitch_min_rad = pmin,
+                .pitch_max_rad = pmax,
+                .current_vfov_rad = cfov,
+                .neutral_vfov_rad = nfov,
+            };
+            LookCommand cmd;
+            mouse_camera_begin_tick(&frame, &cmd);
+            printf("%d %d %llu %.9f %.9f %.9f %.9f %d %d %d %d\n",
+                   (int)cmd.owner, (int)cmd.route, (unsigned long long)cmd.epoch,
+                   cmd.yaw_delta_rad, cmd.pitch_delta_rad,
+                   cmd.target_yaw_rad, cmd.target_pitch_rad,
+                   cmd.compat_rx, cmd.compat_ry,
+                   cmd.suppress_gyro ? 1 : 0, cmd.suppress_gamepad_rstick ? 1 : 0);
+        } else if (line[0] == 'a') {
+            unsigned long long tick = 0, gen = 1;
+            sscanf(line + 1, "%llu %llu", &tick, &gen);
+            mouse_camera_mark_applied(tick, gen);
+            puts("ok");
+        } else if (line[0] == 'd') {
+            MouseCameraDiag diag;
+            mouse_camera_get_diag(&diag);
+            printf("%llu %llu %llu %llu %d %d %d %.6f %.6f\n",
+                   (unsigned long long)diag.epoch,
+                   (unsigned long long)diag.unapplied_count,
+                   (unsigned long long)diag.applied_count,
+                   (unsigned long long)diag.unclassified_count,
+                   (int)diag.owner,
+                   diag.captured ? 1 : 0,
+                   diag.stick_armed ? 1 : 0,
+                   diag.pending_dx, diag.pending_dy);
+        } else if (line[0] == 'n') {
+            int klx, kly, krx, kry, plx, ply, prx, pry;
+            int olx = 0, oly = 0, orx = 0, ory = 0;
+            sscanf(line + 1, "%d %d %d %d %d %d %d %d",
+                   &klx, &kly, &krx, &kry, &plx, &ply, &prx, &pry);
+            mouse_camera_resolve_npad_sticks(klx, kly, krx, kry, plx, ply, prx, pry,
+                                             &olx, &oly, &orx, &ory);
+            printf("%d %d %d %d\n", olx, oly, orx, ory);
+        }
+        fflush(stdout);
+    }
+    return 0;
+}
+'''
+        (cls.work / 'harness.c').write_text(harness, encoding='utf-8')
+        subprocess.run([
+            str(CLANG64 / 'clang.exe'), '-O2', '-pthread',
+            '-I' + str(ROOT / 'src/host'),
+            str(ROOT / 'src/host/mouse_camera.c'),
+            str(cls.work / 'harness.c'),
+            '-o', str(cls.work / 'harness.exe')
+        ], env=env(), check=True)
+        cls.proc = subprocess.Popen(
+            [str(cls.work / 'harness.exe')],
+            env=env(), text=True, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE
+        )
+
+    @classmethod
+    def tearDownClass(cls):
+        cls.proc.stdin.close()
+        cls.proc.wait(timeout=5)
+        for f in cls.work.iterdir():
+            f.unlink()
+        cls.work.rmdir()
+
+    def cmd(self, *args):
+        self.proc.stdin.write(' '.join(map(str, args)) + '\n')
+        self.proc.stdin.flush()
+        line = self.proc.stdout.readline()
+        if not line:
+            raise RuntimeError('Mouse camera harness exited unexpectedly')
+        return line.strip().split()
+
+    def setUp(self):
+        defaults = {
+            'TOTK_CAMERA_MODE': 'mouse',
+            'TOTK_MOUSE_CAMERA_VERSION': '2',
+            'TOTK_MOUSE_BACKEND': 'native',
+            'TOTK_MOUSE_LOOK_DEG_PER_COUNT': '0.04',
+            'TOTK_MOUSE_AIM_MULTIPLIER': '1.0',
+            'TOTK_MOUSE_SCOPE_MULTIPLIER': '1.0',
+            'TOTK_MOUSE_SMOOTH_MS': '0',
+            'TOTK_MOUSE_INVERT_Y': '0',
+            'TOTK_MOUSE_SENSITIVITY': '1.0',
+            'TOTK_MOUSE_TRACE': '0',
+        }
+        for k, v in defaults.items():
+            self.cmd('e', k, v)
+        self.cmd('i')
+        self.cmd('c', 1)
+
+    def tick(self, tick_id, ctx=1, now_ns=1_000_000_000, dt=1.0/60.0, allow=1,
+             yaw=0.0, pitch=0.0, pmin=-1.4, pmax=1.4, cfov=0.8726646, nfov=0.8726646, gen=1):
+        parts = self.cmd('t', tick_id, gen, ctx, now_ns, f'{dt:.8f}', allow,
+                         f'{yaw:.6f}', f'{pitch:.6f}', f'{pmin:.6f}', f'{pmax:.6f}',
+                         f'{cfov:.6f}', f'{nfov:.6f}')
+        return {
+            'owner': int(parts[0]),
+            'route': int(parts[1]),
+            'epoch': int(parts[2]),
+            'dyaw': float(parts[3]),
+            'dpitch': float(parts[4]),
+            'tyaw': float(parts[5]),
+            'tpitch': float(parts[6]),
+            'rx': int(parts[7]),
+            'ry': int(parts[8]),
+            'suppress_gyro': int(parts[9]) == 1,
+            'suppress_rstick': int(parts[10]) == 1,
+        }
+
+    def diag(self):
+        parts = self.cmd('d')
+        return {
+            'epoch': int(parts[0]),
+            'unapplied': int(parts[1]),
+            'applied': int(parts[2]),
+            'unclassified': int(parts[3]),
+            'owner': int(parts[4]),
+            'captured': int(parts[5]) == 1,
+            'stick_armed': int(parts[6]) == 1,
+            'pending_dx': float(parts[7]),
+            'pending_dy': float(parts[8]),
+        }
+
+    def test_same_total_delta_across_different_event_splits_produces_identical_rotation(self):
+        splits = [
+            [(100.0, -50.0)],
+            [(10.0, -5.0)] * 10,
+            [(2.5, -1.25)] * 40,
+            [(0.5, -0.25)] * 200,
+        ]
+        results = []
+        for idx, events in enumerate(splits, start=1):
+            self.cmd('r', 0)
+            self.cmd('c', 1)
+            for i, (dx, dy) in enumerate(events):
+                self.cmd('m', dx, dy, 1_000_000 + i * 1000)
+            res = self.tick(idx)
+            self.cmd('a', idx, 1)
+            results.append((res['dyaw'], res['dpitch']))
+        expected_yaw = math.radians(100.0 * 0.04)
+        expected_pitch = math.radians(50.0 * 0.04)
+        for dyaw, dpitch in results:
+            self.assertAlmostEqual(dyaw, expected_yaw, places=6)
+            self.assertAlmostEqual(dpitch, expected_pitch, places=6)
+
+    def test_fps_independence_30_60_90_120_and_no_acceleration(self):
+        angles = []
+        for tick_id, fps in enumerate((30, 60, 90, 120), start=1):
+            self.cmd('r', 0)
+            self.cmd('c', 1)
+            self.cmd('m', 75.0, -30.0, 10_000_000)
+            res = self.tick(tick_id, dt=1.0 / fps)
+            self.cmd('a', tick_id, 1)
+            angles.append((res['dyaw'], res['dpitch']))
+        base_yaw, base_pitch = angles[0]
+        for dyaw, dpitch in angles[1:]:
+            self.assertAlmostEqual(dyaw, base_yaw, places=7)
+            self.assertAlmostEqual(dpitch, base_pitch, places=7)
+
+    def test_single_unit_and_fractional_deltas_and_invert_y(self):
+        self.cmd('m', 1.0, 1.0, 1000)
+        res = self.tick(1)
+        self.cmd('a', 1, 1)
+        self.assertGreater(res['dyaw'], 0.0)
+        self.assertLess(res['dpitch'], 0.0)  # +dy (down) -> negative pitch when invert_y=0
+
+        # Fractional delta while mouse owns look
+        self.cmd('m', 0.25, -0.5, 2000)
+        res2 = self.tick(2)
+        self.cmd('a', 2, 1)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(res2['dyaw'], math.radians(0.25 * 0.04), places=7)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(res2['dpitch'], math.radians(0.5 * 0.04), places=7)
+
+        # Invert Y flips only vertical sign
+        self.cmd('e', 'TOTK_MOUSE_INVERT_Y', '1')
+        self.cmd('i')
+        self.cmd('c', 1)
+        self.cmd('m', 10.0, 10.0, 3000)
+        res3 = self.tick(3)
+        self.cmd('a', 3, 1)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(res3['dyaw'], math.radians(10.0 * 0.04), places=6)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(res3['dpitch'], math.radians(10.0 * 0.04), places=6)
+
+    def test_diagonal_sweep_preserves_ratio_in_direct_and_compat_stick_modes(self):
+        # Direct mode large diagonal sweep
+        self.cmd('m', 600.0, -300.0, 1000)
+        res = self.tick(1)
+        self.cmd('a', 1, 1)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(res['dyaw'] / res['dpitch'], 2.0, places=5)
+
+        # Menu wheel / compat stick mode large diagonal sweep: radial clamp preserves 2:1 ratio
+        self.cmd('m', 2000.0, -1000.0, 2000)
+        res_wheel = self.tick(2, ctx=5, dt=1.0 / 60.0)  # CAMERA_CTX_MENU_WHEEL = 5
+        self.assertEqual(res_wheel['route'], 2)  # LOOK_ROUTE_COMPAT_STICK
+        mag = math.hypot(res_wheel['rx'], res_wheel['ry'])
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(mag, 32767.0, delta=2.0)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(res_wheel['rx'] / res_wheel['ry'], 2.0, delta=0.01)
+
+    def test_single_consumption_and_unapplied_motion_detection(self):
+        self.cmd('m', 50.0, -25.0, 1000)
+        first = self.tick(10)
+        self.assertEqual(first['route'], 1)
+        self.assertGreater(abs(first['dyaw']), 0.0)
+        # Mark applied on tick 10
+        self.cmd('a', 10, 1)
+        # Second query on same tick 10 returns route=NONE and 0 delta
+        dup = self.tick(10)
+        self.assertEqual(dup['route'], 0)
+        self.assertEqual(dup['dyaw'], 0.0)
+        self.assertEqual(dup['dpitch'], 0.0)
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['unapplied'], 0)
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['applied'], 1)
+
+        # Now test unapplied motion: tick 11 consumes motion, never calls mark_applied, then tick 12 runs
+        self.cmd('m', 40.0, -20.0, 2000)
+        t11 = self.tick(11)
+        self.assertGreater(abs(t11['dyaw']), 0.0)
+        # Advance to tick 12 without marking tick 11 applied:
+        t12 = self.tick(12)
+        self.assertEqual(t12['dyaw'], 0.0)  # Never replays stale motion
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['unapplied'], 1)
+
+    def test_long_frame_preserves_motion_without_replay(self):
+        self.cmd('m', 120.0, -60.0, 1000)
+        res = self.tick(1, dt=0.200)  # 200ms long frame
+        self.cmd('a', 1, 1)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(res['dyaw'], math.radians(120.0 * 0.04), places=6)
+        res_next = self.tick(2, dt=0.016)
+        self.assertEqual(res_next['dyaw'], 0.0)
+
+    def test_stop_has_zero_tail_when_smooth_zero_and_exponential_smoothing_conserves_angle(self):
+        self.cmd('m', 100.0, -50.0, 1000)
+        r1 = self.tick(1)
+        self.cmd('a', 1, 1)
+        self.assertGreater(abs(r1['dyaw']), 0.0)
+        r2 = self.tick(2)
+        self.assertEqual(r2['dyaw'], 0.0)
+        self.assertEqual(r2['dpitch'], 0.0)
+
+        # Enable 10ms smoothing and verify total integrated angle equals unsmoothed target
+        self.cmd('e', 'TOTK_MOUSE_SMOOTH_MS', '10')
+        self.cmd('i')
+        self.cmd('c', 1)
+        self.cmd('m', 100.0, -50.0, 2000)
+        total_yaw = 0.0
+        total_pitch = 0.0
+        for t in range(1, 40):
+            rt = self.tick(t, dt=1.0 / 60.0)
+            if rt['route'] == 1 and (abs(rt['dyaw']) > 0 or abs(rt['dpitch']) > 0):
+                self.cmd('a', t, 1)
+            total_yaw += rt['dyaw']
+            total_pitch += rt['dpitch']
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(total_yaw, math.radians(100.0 * 0.04), places=5)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(total_pitch, math.radians(50.0 * 0.04), places=5)
+
+        # Verify context switch kills remaining smoothing tail immediately
+        self.cmd('m', 100.0, -50.0, 3000)
+        first_step = self.tick(50, ctx=1, dt=0.005)
+        self.cmd('a', 50, 1)
+        self.assertLess(first_step['dyaw'], math.radians(100.0 * 0.04))
+        # Switch context to AIM (ctx=2) with no new mouse input: tail from FREE_ROAM must not bleed
+        switched = self.tick(51, ctx=2, dt=0.005)
+        self.assertEqual(switched['dyaw'], 0.0)
+        self.assertEqual(switched['dpitch'], 0.0)
+
+    def test_focus_loss_f8_and_loading_clear_pending_motion(self):
+        self.cmd('m', 80.0, -40.0, 1000)
+        self.cmd('c', 0)  # F8 release or focus lost
+        self.cmd('c', 1)
+        res = self.tick(1)
+        self.assertEqual(res['dyaw'], 0.0)
+
+        # Loading / cutscene (ctx=9 CAMERA_CTX_DISABLED) drops pending motion
+        self.cmd('m', 80.0, -40.0, 2000)
+        disabled = self.tick(2, ctx=9, allow=0)
+        self.assertEqual(disabled['route'], 0)
+        self.assertEqual(disabled['dyaw'], 0.0)
+        resumed = self.tick(3, ctx=1, allow=1)
+        self.assertEqual(resumed['dyaw'], 0.0)
+
+    def test_stick_and_mouse_ownership_handoff_rules(self):
+        # 1. Start in mouse ownership; stick drift (<25%) does not steal ownership
+        self.cmd('s', 0.18, 0.10, 10_000_000)
+        self.cmd('s', 0.18, 0.10, 100_000_000)
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 0)  # LOOK_OWNER_MOUSE
+
+        # 2. Stick > 25% for < 50ms (e.g. 30ms) does not steal ownership
+        self.cmd('s', 0.50, 0.0, 200_000_000)
+        self.cmd('s', 0.50, 0.0, 230_000_000)
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 0)
+
+        # 3. Stick > 25% for >= 50ms steals ownership to GAMEPAD
+        self.cmd('s', 0.50, 0.0, 255_000_000)
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 1)  # LOOK_OWNER_GAMEPAD
+        r_pad = self.tick(1)
+        self.assertEqual(r_pad['owner'], 1)
+        self.assertFalse(r_pad['suppress_gyro'])
+
+        # 4. Fractional mouse motion (< 1.0 count) does not reclaim yet, but accumulates
+        self.cmd('m', 0.4, 0.3, 260_000_000)  # hypot = 0.5 < 1.0
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 1)
+        # Next motion brings accumulated hypot to >= 1.0: reclaims MOUSE and keeps full accumulated delta!
+        self.cmd('m', 0.4, 0.3, 265_000_000)  # total dx=0.8, dy=0.6 -> hypot = 1.0
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 0)
+        r_mouse = self.tick(2)
+        self.cmd('a', 2, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(r_mouse['owner'], 0)
+        self.assertTrue(r_mouse['suppress_gyro'])
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(r_mouse['dyaw'], math.radians(0.8 * 0.04), places=6)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(r_mouse['dpitch'], math.radians(-0.6 * 0.04), places=6)
+
+        # 5. Stick is STILL tilted (0.50): must NOT reclaim ownership until it returns to <15% for 100ms
+        self.cmd('s', 0.60, 0.0, 300_000_000)
+        self.cmd('s', 0.60, 0.0, 400_000_000)
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 0)  # Still MOUSE!
+
+        # 6. Stick enters <15% for 60ms (not yet 100ms), then pushes >25%: still disarmed!
+        self.cmd('s', 0.05, 0.0, 500_000_000)
+        self.cmd('s', 0.05, 0.0, 560_000_000)
+        self.cmd('s', 0.60, 0.0, 570_000_000)
+        self.cmd('s', 0.60, 0.0, 650_000_000)
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 0)
+
+        # 7. Stick stays <15% for >= 100ms to re-arm, then >25% for >= 50ms to reclaim GAMEPAD
+        self.cmd('s', 0.02, 0.0, 700_000_000)
+        self.cmd('s', 0.02, 0.0, 805_000_000)  # 105ms in neutral -> armed!
+        self.assertTrue(self.diag()['stick_armed'])
+        self.cmd('s', 0.40, 0.0, 810_000_000)
+        self.cmd('s', 0.40, 0.0, 865_000_000)  # 55ms > 25% -> GAMEPAD!
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 1)
+
+    def test_steady_stick_takeover_and_rearm_advance_on_camera_ticks_without_extra_events(self):
+        # Single stick deflection event at t=100ms, held completely steady (no further SDL axis events)
+        self.cmd('s', 0.60, 0.0, 100_000_000)
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 0)
+
+        # At t=130ms (30ms elapsed), camera tick runs -> still MOUSE
+        t1 = self.tick(1, now_ns=130_000_000)
+        self.assertEqual(t1['owner'], 0)
+
+        # At t=150ms (50ms elapsed), camera tick runs -> transitions to GAMEPAD without needing another stick event!
+        t2 = self.tick(2, now_ns=150_000_000)
+        self.assertEqual(t2['owner'], 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 1)
+
+        # Mouse reclaims ownership at t=160ms, disarming the stick
+        self.cmd('m', 2.0, 0.0, 160_000_000)
+        self.assertEqual(self.diag()['owner'], 0)
+        self.assertFalse(self.diag()['stick_armed'])
+
+        # Single stick release to neutral at t=200ms, then untouched (no further SDL axis events)
+        self.cmd('s', 0.0, 0.0, 200_000_000)
+        self.assertFalse(self.diag()['stick_armed'])
+
+        # At t=260ms (60ms in neutral), camera tick runs -> still disarmed
+        self.tick(3, now_ns=260_000_000)
+        self.assertFalse(self.diag()['stick_armed'])
+
+        # At t=300ms (100ms in neutral), camera tick runs -> re-armed without needing another stick event!
+        self.tick(4, now_ns=300_000_000)
+        self.assertTrue(self.diag()['stick_armed'])
+
+    def test_no_axis_mixing_between_devices(self):
+        # Left stick: keyboard has (32767, 0), pad has (0, 20000). Stronger vector wins as a whole!
+        parts = list(map(int, self.cmd('n', 32767, 0, 0, 0, 0, 20000, 15000, -15000)))
+        # Mouse owns look -> right stick is (0, 0), left stick is keyboard's (32767, 0) without mixing pad's Y=20000!
+        self.assertEqual(parts, [32767, 0, 0, 0])
+
+    def test_fov_scaling_in_aim_and_scope_and_lock_on_preservation(self):
+        self.cmd('e', 'TOTK_MOUSE_AIM_MULTIPLIER', '0.8')
+        self.cmd('e', 'TOTK_MOUSE_SCOPE_MULTIPLIER', '0.5')
+        self.cmd('i')
+        self.cmd('c', 1)
+
+        nfov = math.radians(50.0)
+        cfov = math.radians(25.0)
+        fov_scale = math.tan(cfov / 2.0) / math.tan(nfov / 2.0)
+
+        self.cmd('m', 100.0, 0.0, 1000)
+        aim_res = self.tick(1, ctx=2, cfov=cfov, nfov=nfov)  # CAMERA_CTX_AIM = 2
+        self.cmd('a', 1, 1)
+        expected_aim = math.radians(100.0 * 0.04 * fov_scale * 0.8)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(aim_res['dyaw'], expected_aim, places=6)
+
+        self.cmd('m', 100.0, 0.0, 2000)
+        scope_res = self.tick(2, ctx=3, cfov=cfov, nfov=nfov)  # CAMERA_CTX_SCOPE = 3
+        self.cmd('a', 2, 1)
+        expected_scope = math.radians(100.0 * 0.04 * fov_scale * 0.5)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(scope_res['dyaw'], expected_scope, places=6)
+
+        # Lock-on (ctx=4): mouse motion does not break lock-on or emit Npad stick
+        self.cmd('m', 100.0, -50.0, 3000)
+        lock_res = self.tick(3, ctx=4)
+        self.assertEqual(lock_res['route'], 0)
+        self.assertEqual(lock_res['rx'], 0)
+        self.assertEqual(lock_res['ry'], 0)
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    unittest.main(verbosity=2)
